 177:     /** FIX-11H explicit replay-only OLD/NEW selector. Production is not configurable through this mode. */
 178:     public synchronized long start(RegressionTestRunRequest request, ReplayDataSource dataSource) {
 179:         if (request == null || request.symbol() == null || request.symbol().isBlank()
 180:                 || request.startTime() == null || request.endTime() == null
 181:                 || !request.endTime().isAfter(request.startTime())) {
 182:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Symbol, start time and end time are required, and end must be after start.");
 183:         }
 184: 
 185:         Integer activeRuns = jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("""
 186:                 SELECT COUNT(*)
 187:                 FROM analysis_test_run
 188:                 WHERE status IN ('PENDING', 'RUNNING')
 189:                 """, Integer.class);
 190:         if (activeRuns != null && activeRuns > 0) {
 191:             Map<String, Object> active = jdbcTemplate.queryForMap("""
 192:                     SELECT id, test_name, symbol, status, progress_percent, started_at, created_at
 193:                     FROM analysis_test_run
 194:                     WHERE status IN ('PENDING', 'RUNNING')
 195:                     ORDER BY id DESC
 196:                     LIMIT 1
 197:                     """);
 198:             throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
 199:                     "Regression test #" + active.get("id") + " is already " + active.get("status")
 200:                             + " for " + active.get("symbol") + ". Wait for it to finish before starting another run.");
 201:         }
 202: 
 203:         String symbol = request.symbol().trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
 204:         ReplayDataSource effectiveSource = dataSource == null ? ReplayDataSource.DATABASE : dataSource;
 205:         String baseTestName = request.testName() == null || request.testName().isBlank()
 206:                 ? symbol + " regression" : request.testName().trim();
 207:         String testName = (baseTestName + " [" + effectiveSource.name() + "]").substring(
 208:                 0, Math.min(180, baseTestName.length() + effectiveSource.name().length() + 3));
 209: 
 210:         KeyHolder keyHolder = new GeneratedKeyHolder();
 211:         jdbcTemplate.update(connection -> {
 212:             PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement("""
 213:                     INSERT INTO analysis_test_run
 214:                         (test_name, symbol, start_time, end_time, status, progress_percent, current_step)
 215:                     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 'PENDING', 0, 'Queued from Administration')
 216:                     """, Statement.RETURN_GENERATED_KEYS);
 217:             statement.setString(1, testName);
 218:             statement.setString(2, symbol);
 219:             statement.setTimestamp(3, Timestamp.from(request.startTime()));
 220:             statement.setTimestamp(4, Timestamp.from(request.endTime()));
 221:             return statement;
 222:         }, keyHolder);
 223: 
 224:         Number key = keyHolder.getKey();
 225:         if (key == null) {
 226:             throw new IllegalStateException("Could not create regression test run.");
 227:         }
 228:         long id = key.longValue();
 229:         // FIX-122: persist the business-rule revision separately from data-source/experimental modes.
 230:         jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO fix122_replay_revision(test_run_id,enabled) VALUES (?,?)",
 231:                 id, !Boolean.FALSE.equals(request.fix122Enabled()));
 232:         worker.runAsync(id, effectiveSource);
 233:         return id;
 234:     }

 237:     /**
 238:      * Archives one complete replay run before it can be cleared from the active test tables.
 239:      *
 240:      * IMPORTANT regression-history rule (introduced after the ETHUSDT Trade #2 investigation,
 241:      * Aug-2026): test rows are evidence. We must be able to rerun changed production logic without
 242:      * destroying the old pipeline/evidence/health/trade trace that still needs manual analysis.
 243:      * Proven trades remain separate in proven_analyzed_trade and are never moved or deleted here.
 244:      */
 245:     @Transactional
 246:     public synchronized Map<String, Object> archiveRun(long runId, String reason) {
 247:         Integer active = jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM analysis_test_run WHERE id=? AND status IN ('PENDING','RUNNING')", Integer.class, runId);
 248:         if (active != null && active > 0) {
 249:             throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Test #" + runId + " is still running and cannot be archived.");
 250:         }

 254:         if (!existing.isEmpty()) return Map.of("archiveBatchId", existing.get(0).get("id"), "sourceTestRunId", runId, "alreadyArchived", true);
 255:         Map<String,Object> r = rows.get(0);
 256:         KeyHolder kh = new GeneratedKeyHolder();
 257:         jdbcTemplate.update(c -> {
 258:             PreparedStatement ps = c.prepareStatement("INSERT INTO regression_test_archive_batch(source_test_run_id,test_name,symbol,start_time,end_time,source_status,archive_reason) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?)", Statement.RETURN_GENERATED_KEYS);
 259:             ps.setLong(1, runId); ps.setString(2, String.valueOf(r.get("test_name"))); ps.setString(3, String.valueOf(r.get("symbol")));
 260:             ps.setTimestamp(4, (Timestamp) r.get("start_time")); ps.setTimestamp(5, (Timestamp) r.get("end_time"));

 520:         }
 521: 
 522:         Map<String, Object> run = runs.get(0);
 523:         String symbol = String.valueOf(run.get("symbol")).trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
 524:         Timestamp start = (Timestamp) run.get("start_time");
 525:         Timestamp end = (Timestamp) run.get("end_time");
 526:         String normalizedInterval = switch (interval == null ? "5m" : interval.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT)) {
 527:             case "1m", "5m", "1h", "4h" -> interval.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
 528:             default -> "5m";
 529:         };

 531:         List<Map<String, Object>> candles = candleReader(jdbcTemplate).queryForList("""

 604:     public Map<String, Object> markProvenSuccess(long runId, long tradeId) {
 605:         List<Map<String, Object>> rows = jdbcTemplate.queryForList("""
 606:                 SELECT id, test_run_id, symbol, entry_time, entry_price, exit_time, exit_price, exit_reason,
 607:                        realized_pnl_usdt, realized_pnl_percent, position_percent

 613:         jdbcTemplate.update("""
 614:                 INSERT INTO proven_analyzed_trade
 615:                     (source_test_run_id, source_trade_id, symbol, entry_time, entry_price, exit_time, exit_price,
 616:                      exit_reason, realized_pnl_usdt, realized_pnl_percent, position_percent)
 617:                 VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)

 621:                     position_percent=VALUES(position_percent), marked_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
 622:                 """,
 623:                 runId, tradeId, t.get("symbol"), t.get("entry_time"), t.get("entry_price"), t.get("exit_time"),
 624:                 t.get("exit_price"), t.get("exit_reason"), t.get("realized_pnl_usdt"), t.get("realized_pnl_percent"),
 625:                 t.get("position_percent"));
 626:         // FIX-122: snapshot before a later Replay purge can remove/reuse this run identity.
 627:         Long provenId=jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT id FROM proven_analyzed_trade WHERE source_test_run_id=? AND source_trade_id=?",Long.class,runId,tradeId);

 629:                 ((Timestamp)t.get("entry_time")).toInstant().minusSeconds(1800),((Timestamp)t.get("exit_time")).toInstant());
 630:         return Map.of("saved", true, "runId", runId, "tradeId", tradeId);
 631:     }

 655:     /**
 656:      * Archive only one reviewed execution leg. This is intentionally separate from
 657:      * regression-run archiving: a reviewer can preserve the BUY or SELL point on its
 658:      * own without freezing/copying the complete test run. Clear Data still keeps the
 659:      * existing full-run safety archive so replay diagnostics remain recoverable.
 660:      */
 661:     @Transactional
 662:     public Map<String, Object> archiveProvenTradeLeg(long provenTradeId, String requestedSide) {
 663:         String side = requestedSide == null ? "" : requestedSide.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
 664:         if (!side.equals("BUY") && !side.equals("SELL")) {
 665:             throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Archive side must be BUY or SELL.");
 666:         }
 667:         List<Map<String, Object>> rows = jdbcTemplate.queryForList("""
 668:                 SELECT id, source_test_run_id, source_trade_id, symbol, entry_time, entry_price, exit_time, exit_price,
 669:                        exit_reason, realized_pnl_usdt, realized_pnl_percent
 670:                 FROM proven_analyzed_trade
 671:                 WHERE id=?
 672:                 """, provenTradeId);
 673:         if (rows.isEmpty()) throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Proven trade not found.");
 674:         Map<String, Object> trade = rows.get(0);
 675:         Object time = side.equals("BUY") ? trade.get("entry_time") : trade.get("exit_time");
 676:         Object price = side.equals("BUY") ? trade.get("entry_price") : trade.get("exit_price");
 677:         if (time == null || price == null) {
 678:             throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, side + " leg is not available for this proven trade.");
 679:         }
 680:         jdbcTemplate.update("""
 681:                 INSERT INTO proven_trade_leg_archive
 682:                     (proven_trade_id, source_test_run_id, source_trade_id, symbol, side, execution_time, execution_price,
 683:                      exit_reason, realized_pnl_usdt, realized_pnl_percent)
 684:                 VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
 685:                 ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE execution_time=VALUES(execution_time), execution_price=VALUES(execution_price),
 686:                     exit_reason=VALUES(exit_reason), realized_pnl_usdt=VALUES(realized_pnl_usdt),
 687:                     realized_pnl_percent=VALUES(realized_pnl_percent), archived_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
 688:                 """, provenTradeId, trade.get("source_test_run_id"), trade.get("source_trade_id"), trade.get("symbol"),
 689:                 side, time, price, side.equals("SELL") ? trade.get("exit_reason") : null,
 690:                 side.equals("SELL") ? trade.get("realized_pnl_usdt") : null,
 691:                 side.equals("SELL") ? trade.get("realized_pnl_percent") : null);
 692:         return Map.of("archived", true, "provenTradeId", provenTradeId, "side", side);
 693:     }

  46:     /**
  47:      * Replay warm-up is context-only. It gives the production analysis/execution services
  48:      * the same already-closed 1m/5m/1h history they would have had at the requested start
  49:      * without allowing wallet executions before the requested test window.
  50:      */
  51:     private static final Duration REPLAY_CONTEXT_WARMUP = Duration.ofHours(3);
  52:

  66:     // FIX-087: heartbeat age alone cannot prove that a long replay worker has died.
  67:     // Some deterministic replay stages can legitimately run for more than the stale-heartbeat
  68:     // threshold without returning to updateRun(). Keep atomic in-JVM ownership so Resume cannot
  69:     // launch a second worker for the same durable run while the first worker is still executing.
  70:     private final Set<Long> activeRunIds = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
  71: 
  72:     // FIX-090: cooperative stop requests are tracked separately from active ownership. A Stop Test
  73:     // request never pretends the worker is gone; Delete Data stays blocked until runAsync reaches
  74:     // finally and removes the run from activeRunIds.
  75:     private final Set<Long> stopRequestedRunIds = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
  76: 
  77:     public boolean isActive(long runId) {
  78:         return activeRunIds.contains(runId);
  79:     }
  80: 
  81:     public boolean hasActiveRuns() {
  82:         return !activeRunIds.isEmpty();
  83:     }
  84: 
  85:     public boolean requestStop(long runId) {
  86:         if (!activeRunIds.contains(runId)) return false;
  87:         stopRequestedRunIds.add(runId);
  88:         return true;
  89:     }
  90: 
  91:     public boolean isStopRequested(long runId) {
  92:         return stopRequestedRunIds.contains(runId);
  93:     }
  94: 
  95:     private void checkStopRequested(long runId) {
  96:         if (stopRequestedRunIds.contains(runId)) throw new ReplayCancellationException(runId);
  97:     }
  98:

 509:     private List<Candle> candles(String symbol, String interval, Instant start, Instant end) {
 510:         return candleRepository.findBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndOpenTimeBetweenOrderByOpenTimeAsc(
 511:                 symbol, interval, start, end);
 512:     }
 513: 
 514:     private int verifyEventResolution(long runId, String symbol, String interval, List<Candle> candles,
 515:                                       int progressStart, int progressEnd) {
 516:         int resolved = 0;
 517:         int size = candles.size();
 518:         for (int i = 0; i < size; i++) {
 519:             checkStopRequested(runId);
 520:             Candle candle = candles.get(i);
 521:             List<Candle> latestAtEvent = candleRepository.findClosedCandlesAtOrBefore(
 522:                     symbol, interval, candle.getOpenTime(), PageRequest.of(0, 1));
 523:             if (!latestAtEvent.isEmpty() && candle.getOpenTime().equals(latestAtEvent.get(0).getOpenTime())) {
 524:                 resolved++;
 525:             }
 526:             if (i % 25 == 0 && size > 0) {
 527:                 int p = progressStart + (int) Math.round((progressEnd - progressStart) * (i / (double) size));
 528:                 updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", p,
 529:                         "Replaying " + interval + " candle " + (i + 1) + "/" + size, null);
 530:             }
 531:         }
 532:         return resolved;
 533:     }

 535:     private FreshReplayStats generateFreshSignals(
 536:             long runId,
 537:             String symbol,
 538:             Instant executionStart,
 539:             Instant executionEnd,
 540:             List<Candle> oneMinuteCandles,
 541:             List<Candle> fiveMinuteCandles,
 542:             List<Candle> oneHourCandles,
 543:             String btcSymbol,
 544:             List<Candle> btcOneMinuteCandles,
 545:             List<Candle> btcFiveMinuteCandles,
 546:             List<Candle> btcOneHourCandles,
 547:             ReplayDataSource dataSource,
 548:             ReplayDataset replayDataset
 549:     ) {
 550:         List<ReplayCandle> timeline = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 551:         oneMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "1m", c, true)));
 552:         fiveMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "5m", c, true)));
 553:         oneHourCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "1h", c, true)));
 554:         btcOneMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "1m", c, false)));
 555:         btcFiveMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "5m", c, false)));
 556:         btcOneHourCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "1h", c, false)));
 557:         timeline.sort(java.util.Comparator
 558:                 .comparing((ReplayCandle rc) -> rc.candle().getOpenTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(rc.interval())))
 559:                 .thenComparingInt(rc -> intervalOrder(rc.interval()))
 560:                 .thenComparingInt(rc -> rc.primary() ? 1 : 0));
 561: 
 562:         int oneMinuteSignals = 0, fiveMinuteSignals = 0, oneHourSignals = 0;
 563:         int oneMinuteBuys = 0, fiveMinuteBuys = 0, oneHourBuys = 0;
 564:         int buys = 0, watches = 0, sells = 0, strongSells = 0, errors = 0;
 565:         java.util.List<TradeSignal> generatedSignals = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 566: 
 567:         // FIX-11L: Replay-only deep profiling for the Generate fresh signals stage.
 568:         // These monotonic timers observe existing calls only; they do not alter inputs,
 569:         // call ordering, persistence, thresholds, or any Production trading behavior.
 570:         ReplayFreshSignalProfiler profiler = new ReplayFreshSignalProfiler(runId, dataSource);
 571:         // FIX-11M: Enable aggregate timing only for this Replay worker thread.
 572:         // The shared AnalysisService decision path is unchanged; this is observation-only.
 573:         analysisService.beginReplayAnalysisProfiling(runId);
 574:         try (ExecutionReplayScope.Scope ignored = replayScope.open(runId, List.of(), o -> {})) {
 575:         // FIX-043 Replay parity: fresh Replay has always generated technical snapshots/signals
 576:         // chronologically for EVERY closed candle in the timeline. Production now restores the
 577:         // same no-gap contract with asynchronous close-event processing plus chronological recovery.
 578:         // Do not "optimize" Replay by sampling every Nth candle or by copying the production recovery
 579:         // scheduler cadence; that would recreate the exact 1m->~5m blind-gap incident FIX-043 fixes.
 580:         for (int index = 0; index < timeline.size(); index++) {
 581:             checkStopRequested(runId);
 582:             ReplayCandle replay = timeline.get(index);
 583:             Candle candle = replay.candle();
 584:             Instant evaluationTime = candle.getOpenTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(replay.interval()));
 585:             boolean inRequestedWindow = !evaluationTime.isBefore(executionStart) && !evaluationTime.isAfter(executionEnd);
 586:             try {
 587:                 long operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 588:                 java.util.Optional<IndicatorSnapshot> snapshot;
 589:                 try {
 590:                     snapshot = dataSource == ReplayDataSource.DATASET
 591:                             ? technicalIndicatorService.calculateSnapshotForRegression(
 592:                                     replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle.getOpenTime(), replayDataset)
 593:                             : technicalIndicatorService.calculateSnapshotForRegression(
 594:                                     replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle.getOpenTime());
 595:                 } finally {
 596:                     profiler.recordSnapshot(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
 597:                 }
 598:                 if (snapshot.isEmpty()) {
 599:                     if (inRequestedWindow) {
 600:                         errors++;
 601:                         saveGenerationError(runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle,
 602:                                 "Not enough historical candles to calculate the technical snapshot");
 603:                     }
 604:                     continue;
 605:                 }
 606: 
 607:                 // Do not generate a signal whose candle would only have closed after the
 608:                 // requested test end. Warm-up rows before executionStart are intentionally
 609:                 // generated for as-of context, but are not counted or displayed as test output.
 610:                 if (evaluationTime.isAfter(executionEnd)) continue;
 611: 
 612:                 replayScope.reference(evaluationTime);
 613:                 operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 614:                 TradeSignal generated;
 615:                 try {
 616:                     generated = analysisService.analyzeForRegression(snapshot.get(), evaluationTime);
 617:                 } finally {
 618:                     profiler.recordAnalysis(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
 619:                 }
 620:                 replayScope.appendSignal(generated);
 621:                 SignalDecision decision = generated.getDecision();
 622:                 if (replay.primary()) generatedSignals.add(generated);
 623: 
 624:                 if (replay.primary() && inRequestedWindow) {
 625:                     if ("1m".equals(replay.interval())) { oneMinuteSignals++; if (isBuy(decision)) oneMinuteBuys++; }
 626:                     else if ("5m".equals(replay.interval())) { fiveMinuteSignals++; if (isBuy(decision)) fiveMinuteBuys++; }
 627:                     else if ("1h".equals(replay.interval())) { oneHourSignals++; if (isBuy(decision)) oneHourBuys++; }
 628: 
 629:                     if (decision == SignalDecision.BUY || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY) buys++;
 630:                     else if (decision == SignalDecision.WATCH) watches++;
 631:                     else if (decision == SignalDecision.SELL) sells++;
 632:                     else if (decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL) strongSells++;
 633: 
 634:                     operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 635:                     try {
 636:                         jdbcTemplate.update("""
 637:                             INSERT INTO analysis_test_signal
 638:                                 (test_run_id, source_signal_id, replay_generated, symbol, interval_code, candle_open_time,
 639:                                  generated_at, latest_price, original_decision, final_decision,
 640:                                  execution_effective_decision, total_score, confidence_score, raw_confidence_score,
 641:                                  effective_confidence_score, primary_blocking_stage, detected_regime, candidate_regime,
 642:                                  confirmed_regime, regime_candidate_count, entry_authority, entry_authority_max_position_percent,
 643:                                  trend_score, volume_score, momentum_score, decision_authority_corrected, generation_error)
 644:                             VALUES (?, NULL, 1, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, NULL)
 645:                             """,
 646:                             runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), Timestamp.from(candle.getOpenTime()),
 647:                             Timestamp.from(evaluationTime), generated.getLatestPrice(),
 648:                             generated.getOriginalDecision() == null ? null : generated.getOriginalDecision().name(),
 649:                             decision == null ? null : decision.name(),
 650:                             decision == null ? null : decision.name(),
 651:                             generated.getTotalScore(), generated.getConfidenceScore(), generated.getRawConfidenceScore(),

 663:                     // FIX-069: Persist the exact production TradeSignal shape as replay output.
 664:                     // analysis_test_signal remains for backward-compatible diagnostics, while

 666:                     // FIX-112D: Replay lineage uses exact market-candle identity, never
 667:                     // generated_at or nearest-time matching. A legitimate Replay-only

 669:                     operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 670:                     Long sourceSignalId;
 671:                     try {

 683:                         persistTradeSignalTest(runId, generated, sourceSignalId, null);
 684:                     } finally {
 685:                         profiler.recordTradeSignalTestPersistence(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
 686:                     }
 687:                 }
 688:             } catch (ReplayCancellationException stop) {
 689:                 throw stop;
 690:             } catch (Exception exception) {
 691:                 if (replay.primary() && inRequestedWindow) {
 692:                     errors++;
 693:                     saveGenerationError(runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle, errorDetail(exception, 900));
 694:                 }
 695:                 log.warn("Regression fresh-signal generation failed: run={}, symbol={}, interval={}, candle={}",
 696:                         runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle.getOpenTime(), exception);
 697:             }
 698: 
 699:             if (index % 10 == 0 && !timeline.isEmpty()) {
 700:                 int progress = 43 + (int) Math.round(30d * index / timeline.size());
 701:                 long progressStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 702:                 try {
 703:                     updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", Math.min(73, progress),
 704:                             "Generating fresh signal " + (index + 1) + "/" + timeline.size(), null);
 705:                 } finally {
 706:                     profiler.recordProgressUpdate(System.nanoTime() - progressStartedNs);
 707:                 }
 708:             }
 709:         }

  46:     /**
  47:      * Replay warm-up is context-only. It gives the production analysis/execution services
  48:      * the same already-closed 1m/5m/1h history they would have had at the requested start
  49:      * without allowing wallet executions before the requested test window.
  50:      */
  51:     private static final Duration REPLAY_CONTEXT_WARMUP = Duration.ofHours(3);
  52: 
  53:     private final JdbcTemplate jdbcTemplate;

  66:     // FIX-087: heartbeat age alone cannot prove that a long replay worker has died.
  67:     // Some deterministic replay stages can legitimately run for more than the stale-heartbeat
  68:     // threshold without returning to updateRun(). Keep atomic in-JVM ownership so Resume cannot
  69:     // launch a second worker for the same durable run while the first worker is still executing.
  70:     private final Set<Long> activeRunIds = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
  71: 
  72:     // FIX-090: cooperative stop requests are tracked separately from active ownership. A Stop Test
  73:     // request never pretends the worker is gone; Delete Data stays blocked until runAsync reaches
  74:     // finally and removes the run from activeRunIds.
  75:     private final Set<Long> stopRequestedRunIds = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
  76: 
  77:     public boolean isActive(long runId) {
  78:         return activeRunIds.contains(runId);
  79:     }
  80: 
  81:     public boolean hasActiveRuns() {
  82:         return !activeRunIds.isEmpty();
  83:     }
  84: 
  85:     public boolean requestStop(long runId) {
  86:         if (!activeRunIds.contains(runId)) return false;
  87:         stopRequestedRunIds.add(runId);
  88:         return true;
  89:     }
  90: 
  91:     public boolean isStopRequested(long runId) {
  92:         return stopRequestedRunIds.contains(runId);
  93:     }
  94: 
  95:     private void checkStopRequested(long runId) {
  96:         if (stopRequestedRunIds.contains(runId)) throw new ReplayCancellationException(runId);
  97:     }

  99:     @Async
 100:     public void runAsync(long runId, ReplayDataSource dataSource) {
 101:         // FIX-087: close the race at worker entry as well. Even if two callers schedule the same
 102:         // run almost simultaneously, only one may execute the deterministic replay pipeline.
 103:         if (!activeRunIds.add(runId)) {
 104:             log.warn("FIX-087: replay run {} already has an active worker; duplicate execution skipped", runId);
 105:             return;
 106:         }
 107:         // FIX-11J: Replay-only observational timers. The worker pipeline below is intentionally
 108:         // left in its existing order; these locals only capture elapsed monotonic time and are
 109:         // persisted once in finally so diagnostics cannot steer or fail Replay business logic.
 110:         final long replayTotalStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 111:         Long loadHistoricalDataNs = null;
 112:         Long verifyEventResolutionNs = null;
 113:         Long buildReplayDatasetNs = null;
 114:         Long generateFreshSignalsNs = null;

 514:     private int verifyEventResolution(long runId, String symbol, String interval, List<Candle> candles,
 515:                                       int progressStart, int progressEnd) {
 516:         int resolved = 0;
 517:         int size = candles.size();
 518:         for (int i = 0; i < size; i++) {
 519:             checkStopRequested(runId);
 520:             Candle candle = candles.get(i);
 521:             List<Candle> latestAtEvent = candleRepository.findClosedCandlesAtOrBefore(
 522:                     symbol, interval, candle.getOpenTime(), PageRequest.of(0, 1));
 523:             if (!latestAtEvent.isEmpty() && candle.getOpenTime().equals(latestAtEvent.get(0).getOpenTime())) {
 524:                 resolved++;
 525:             }
 526:             if (i % 25 == 0 && size > 0) {
 527:                 int p = progressStart + (int) Math.round((progressEnd - progressStart) * (i / (double) size));
 528:                 updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", p,
 529:                         "Replaying " + interval + " candle " + (i + 1) + "/" + size, null);
 530:             }
 531:         }
 532:         return resolved;
 533:     }

 535:     private FreshReplayStats generateFreshSignals(
 536:             long runId,
 537:             String symbol,
 538:             Instant executionStart,
 539:             Instant executionEnd,

 541:             List<Candle> fiveMinuteCandles,

 546:             List<Candle> btcOneHourCandles,
 547:             ReplayDataSource dataSource,
 548:             ReplayDataset replayDataset
 549:     ) {
 550:         List<ReplayCandle> timeline = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 551:         oneMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "1m", c, true)));
 552:         fiveMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "5m", c, true)));
 553:         oneHourCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "1h", c, true)));
 554:         btcOneMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "1m", c, false)));
 555:         btcFiveMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "5m", c, false)));
 556:         btcOneHourCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "1h", c, false)));
 557:         timeline.sort(java.util.Comparator
 558:                 .comparing((ReplayCandle rc) -> rc.candle().getOpenTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(rc.interval())))
 559:                 .thenComparingInt(rc -> intervalOrder(rc.interval()))
 560:                 .thenComparingInt(rc -> rc.primary() ? 1 : 0));
 561: 
 562:         int oneMinuteSignals = 0, fiveMinuteSignals = 0, oneHourSignals = 0;
 563:         int oneMinuteBuys = 0, fiveMinuteBuys = 0, oneHourBuys = 0;
 564:         int buys = 0, watches = 0, sells = 0, strongSells = 0, errors = 0;
 565:         java.util.List<TradeSignal> generatedSignals = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 566: 
 567:         // FIX-11L: Replay-only deep profiling for the Generate fresh signals stage.
 568:         // These monotonic timers observe existing calls only; they do not alter inputs,
 569:         // call ordering, persistence, thresholds, or any Production trading behavior.

 575:         // FIX-043 Replay parity: fresh Replay has always generated technical snapshots/signals
 576:         // chronologically for EVERY closed candle in the timeline. Production now restores the
 577:         // same no-gap contract with asynchronous close-event processing plus chronological recovery.
 578:         // Do not "optimize" Replay by sampling every Nth candle or by copying the production recovery
 579:         // scheduler cadence; that would recreate the exact 1m->~5m blind-gap incident FIX-043 fixes.
 580:         for (int index = 0; index < timeline.size(); index++) {

 593:                             : technicalIndicatorService.calculateSnapshotForRegression(
 594:                                     replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle.getOpenTime());
 595:                 } finally {
 596:                     profiler.recordSnapshot(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
 597:                 }
 598:                 if (snapshot.isEmpty()) {
 599:                     if (inRequestedWindow) {
 600:                         errors++;
 601:                         saveGenerationError(runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle,
 602:                                 "Not enough historical candles to calculate the technical snapshot");
 603:                     }
 604:                     continue;
 605:                 }
 606:

 610:                 if (evaluationTime.isAfter(executionEnd)) continue;
 611: 
 612:                 replayScope.reference(evaluationTime);
 613:                 operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();

 628:

 133:     public java.util.Optional<PriceEvent> findLatestAtOrBefore(String rawSymbol, Instant reference) {
 134:         String symbol = rawSymbol == null ? "" : rawSymbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);

 136:                 SELECT observed_at, price, source
 137:                 FROM market_price_event
 138:                 WHERE delivery_status IN ('LEGACY','APPLIED') AND symbol = ? AND observed_at <= ?
 139:                 ORDER BY observed_at DESC, id DESC
 140:                 LIMIT 1
 141:                 """, (rs, rowNum) -> new PriceEvent(

 148:     /** FIX-132: known skipped/unconfirmed collector delivery must not silently
 149:      * become legacy candle-price fallback. Historical pre-collector windows retain
 150:      * their established fallback. This reads immutable evidence, never live claims. */
 151:     public void assertReplayEvidence(String symbol,Instant from,Instant to) {
 152:         Long pending=jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM market_price_event WHERE symbol=? AND observed_at BETWEEN ? AND ? AND delivery_status='PENDING'",
 153:             Long.class,symbol,Timestamp.from(from),Timestamp.from(to));
 154:         if(pending!=null && pending>0)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 Replay contains unconfirmed Production price outcomes; reconcile before claiming parity");
 155:         // FIX-153 OFF Replay never queries collector-era activation tables.
 156:         if("OFF".equals(mode))return;
 157:         Long cutovers=jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM shared_market_cutover_history WHERE symbol=? AND cutover_at<=?",Long.class,symbol,Timestamp.from(to));
 158:         if(cutovers!=null && cutovers>0) {
 159:             Long applied=jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM market_price_event WHERE symbol=? AND observed_at BETWEEN ? AND ? AND delivery_status IN ('LEGACY','APPLIED')",
 160:                 Long.class,symbol,Timestamp.from(from),Timestamp.from(to));
 161:             if(applied==null || applied==0)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 no applied price evidence in collector-era Replay window; signal-price fallback is not Production reproduction");
 162:         }
 163:     }

 166:     public java.util.Map<String,Long> replayOutcomeCounts(String symbol,Instant from,Instant to){

 169:             (org.springframework.jdbc.core.RowCallbackHandler)rs->result.put(rs.getString(1),rs.getLong(2)),symbol,Timestamp.from(from),Timestamp.from(to));
 170:         return result;
 171:     }

  32:     private List<Candle> read(String tail,Object... args) {
  33:         Object[] converted=Arrays.stream(args).map(v->v instanceof Instant i ? Timestamp.from(i) : v).toArray();
  34:         List<Candle> rows=source.reader().query("SELECT * FROM candle WHERE "+tail,MAPPER,converted);
  35:         com.crypto.shared.CandleInputAudit.capture(tail,rows);
  36:         return rows;
  37:     }

  76:     public List<Candle> findClosedCandlesMissingAnalysisThrough(String s,String i,Instant a,Instant b,Pageable p) {
  77:         // Shared credentials never need access to Trader's indicators/signals. Fetch
  78:         // local identities separately, then paginate AFTER applying the missing test.
  79:         Set<Instant> indicators=new HashSet<>(local.query("SELECT candle_open_time FROM technical_indicator WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND candle_open_time BETWEEN ? AND ?",
  80:             (r,n)->r.getTimestamp(1).toInstant(),s,i,Timestamp.from(a),Timestamp.from(b)));
  81:         Set<Instant> signals=new HashSet<>(local.query("SELECT candle_open_time FROM trade_signal WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND candle_open_time BETWEEN ? AND ?",
  82:             (r,n)->r.getTimestamp(1).toInstant(),s,i,Timestamp.from(a),Timestamp.from(b)));
  83:         long started=System.nanoTime();
  84:         var candles=findBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndClosedTrueAndOpenTimeBetweenOrderByOpenTimeAsc(s,i,a,b);
  85:         long fetchMs=(System.nanoTime()-started)/1_000_000;
  86:         var result=candles.stream()
  87:             .filter(c->!indicators.contains(c.getOpenTime()) || !signals.contains(c.getOpenTime()))
  88:             .skip(p.isUnpaged()?0:p.getOffset()).limit(p.isUnpaged()?Long.MAX_VALUE:p.getPageSize()).toList();
  89:         // Measurement only. Fetch includes pool acquisition, transfer and mapping;
  90:         // it is not labelled database execution or commit time. Filtering unchanged.
  91:         if(source.enabled()) {
  92:             String detail="fetched="+candles.size()+", returned="+result.size()+", fetchIncludingPoolMs="+fetchMs+", pool="+source.poolPressure();
  93:             log.info("[FIX-132][RECOVERY_SCAN] symbol={}, interval={}, from={}, through={}, {}",s,i,a,b,detail);
  94:             try { local.update("INSERT INTO shared_market_health(component,status,detail) VALUES(?,'MEASURED',?) ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE status=VALUES(status),detail=VALUES(detail)","RECOVERY:"+s+":"+i,detail); }
  95:             catch(RuntimeException unavailable) {log.warn("[FIX-132][RECOVERY_METRIC_NOT_PERSISTED] symbol={}, interval={}",s,i,unavailable);}
  96:         }
  97:         return result;
  98:     }
  99:     public int upsert(String symbol,String intervalCode,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime,
 100:         BigDecimal openPrice,BigDecimal highPrice,BigDecimal lowPrice,BigDecimal closePrice,
 101:         BigDecimal volume,BigDecimal quoteAssetVolume,Long numberOfTrades,BigDecimal takerBuyBaseVolume,
 102:         BigDecimal takerBuyQuoteVolume,boolean closed) {
 103:         source.requireLocalWriter();
 104:         return write(symbol,intervalCode,openTime,closeTime,openPrice,highPrice,lowPrice,closePrice,
 105:             volume,quoteAssetVolume,numberOfTrades,takerBuyBaseVolume,takerBuyQuoteVolume,closed,"BACKFILL",null);
 106:     }
 107:     /** FIX-153: result is valid only after the caller's INPUT transaction commits.
 108:      * Token read-back compares immutable receipt identity, never a racy pre-read of closed state. */
 109:     public Transition upsertRuntime(String symbol,String intervalCode,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime,
 110:         BigDecimal openPrice,BigDecimal highPrice,BigDecimal lowPrice,BigDecimal closePrice,
 111:         BigDecimal volume,BigDecimal quoteAssetVolume,Long numberOfTrades,BigDecimal takerBuyBaseVolume,
 112:         BigDecimal takerBuyQuoteVolume,boolean closed,String token) {
 113:         source.requireLocalWriter();
 114:         write(symbol,intervalCode,openTime,closeTime,openPrice,highPrice,lowPrice,closePrice,
 115:             volume,quoteAssetVolume,numberOfTrades,takerBuyBaseVolume,takerBuyQuoteVolume,closed,"RUNTIME",closed?token:null);
 116:         return local.queryForObject("SELECT closed,close_time,close_transition_token,ingestion_source FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND open_time=?",
 117:             // Provenance deliberately does not participate here. A legacy NULL row that
 118:             // the live stream closes is a genuine close transition; BACKFILL never writes
 119:             // a token, so it cannot manufacture a dispatchable transition.
 120:             (rs,n)->new Transition(closed && token.equals(rs.getString(3)),
 121:                 rs.getTimestamp(2).toInstant(),rs.getString(3),rs.getBoolean(1)),symbol,intervalCode,Timestamp.from(openTime));
 122:     }
 123:     /** `alreadyClosed` is diagnostic only; the matching token is the sole dispatch authority. */
 124:     public record Transition(boolean transitionedToClosed,Instant closeTime,String token,boolean alreadyClosed) {}

 126:     /**
 127:      * Authoritative recovery is intentionally update-only.  Unlike ordinary
 128:      * REST backfill it can never insert an absent identity and it never makes a
 129:      * runtime close token.  This keeps a late recovery from stealing a normal
 130:      * live close admission.
 131:      */
 132:     public boolean finalizeExistingOpenFromAuthoritative(String symbol,String intervalCode,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime,
 133:         BigDecimal openPrice,BigDecimal highPrice,BigDecimal lowPrice,BigDecimal closePrice,
 134:         BigDecimal volume,BigDecimal quoteAssetVolume,Long numberOfTrades,BigDecimal takerBuyBaseVolume,
 135:         BigDecimal takerBuyQuoteVolume) {
 136:         source.requireLocalWriter();
 137:         return local.update("""
 138:             UPDATE candle SET close_time=?,open_price=?,high_price=?,low_price=?,close_price=?,volume=?,
 139:               quote_asset_volume=?,number_of_trades=?,taker_buy_base_volume=?,taker_buy_quote_volume=?,
 140:               closed=1,updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
 141:             WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND open_time=? AND closed=0
 142:               AND (ingestion_source IS NULL OR ingestion_source<>'BACKFILL')
 143:             """, Timestamp.from(closeTime),openPrice,highPrice,lowPrice,closePrice,volume,quoteAssetVolume,
 144:             numberOfTrades,takerBuyBaseVolume,takerBuyQuoteVolume,symbol,intervalCode,Timestamp.from(openTime)) == 1;
 145:     }
 146: 
 147:     /**
 148:      * Exact authoritative gap repair is pure insert-if-absent.  Do not replace
 149:      * this with ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE: a live row may arrive while REST is in
 150:      * flight and must remain untouched so its normal close can own the token.
 151:      */
 152:     public boolean insertAuthoritativeGapBackfill(String symbol,String intervalCode,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime,
 153:         BigDecimal openPrice,BigDecimal highPrice,BigDecimal lowPrice,BigDecimal closePrice,
 154:         BigDecimal volume,BigDecimal quoteAssetVolume,Long numberOfTrades,BigDecimal takerBuyBaseVolume,
 155:         BigDecimal takerBuyQuoteVolume) {
 156:         source.requireLocalWriter();
 157:         try {
 158:             return local.update("""
 159:                 INSERT INTO candle(symbol,interval_code,open_time,close_time,open_price,high_price,low_price,close_price,
 160:                     volume,quote_asset_volume,number_of_trades,taker_buy_base_volume,taker_buy_quote_volume,
 161:                     closed,ingestion_source,close_transition_token,created_at,updated_at)
 162:                 VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,1,'BACKFILL',NULL,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6))
 163:                 """,symbol,intervalCode,Timestamp.from(openTime),Timestamp.from(closeTime),openPrice,highPrice,lowPrice,
 164:                 closePrice,volume,quoteAssetVolume,numberOfTrades,takerBuyBaseVolume,takerBuyQuoteVolume) == 1;
 165:         } catch (org.springframework.dao.DuplicateKeyException duplicate) {
 166:             return false;
 167:         }
 168:     }
 169:     private int write(String symbol,String intervalCode,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime,
 170:         BigDecimal openPrice,BigDecimal highPrice,BigDecimal lowPrice,BigDecimal closePrice,
 171:         BigDecimal volume,BigDecimal quoteAssetVolume,Long numberOfTrades,BigDecimal takerBuyBaseVolume,
 172:         BigDecimal takerBuyQuoteVolume,boolean closed,String provenance,String token) {
 173:         String duplicateClause = "BACKFILL".equals(provenance) ? """
 174:             ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE
 175:                 -- REST/backfill is authoritative enough to finish an interrupted open
 176:                 -- candle, but never changes a completed candle, provenance or token.
 177:                 close_time=IF(closed=1,close_time,VALUES(close_time)),
 178:                 open_price=IF(closed=1,open_price,VALUES(open_price)),
 179:                 high_price=IF(closed=1,high_price,VALUES(high_price)),
 180:                 low_price=IF(closed=1,low_price,VALUES(low_price)),
 181:                 close_price=IF(closed=1,close_price,VALUES(close_price)),
 182:                 volume=IF(closed=1,volume,VALUES(volume)),
 183:                 quote_asset_volume=IF(closed=1,quote_asset_volume,VALUES(quote_asset_volume)),
 184:                 number_of_trades=IF(closed=1,number_of_trades,VALUES(number_of_trades)),
 185:                 taker_buy_base_volume=IF(closed=1,taker_buy_base_volume,VALUES(taker_buy_base_volume)),
 186:                 taker_buy_quote_volume=IF(closed=1,taker_buy_quote_volume,VALUES(taker_buy_quote_volume)),
 187:                 updated_at=IF(closed=1,updated_at,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)),
 188:                 closed=GREATEST(closed,VALUES(closed))
 189:             """ : """
 190:             ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE
 191:                 close_time=IF(closed=1,close_time,VALUES(close_time)),
 192:                 open_price=IF(closed=1,open_price,VALUES(open_price)),
 193:                 high_price=IF(closed=1,high_price,VALUES(high_price)),
 194:                 low_price=IF(closed=1,low_price,VALUES(low_price)),
 195:                 close_price=IF(closed=1,close_price,VALUES(close_price)),
 196:                 volume=IF(closed=1,volume,VALUES(volume)),
 197:                 quote_asset_volume=IF(closed=1,quote_asset_volume,VALUES(quote_asset_volume)),
 198:                 number_of_trades=IF(closed=1,number_of_trades,VALUES(number_of_trades)),
 199:                 taker_buy_base_volume=IF(closed=1,taker_buy_base_volume,VALUES(taker_buy_base_volume)),
 200:                 taker_buy_quote_volume=IF(closed=1,taker_buy_quote_volume,VALUES(taker_buy_quote_volume)),
 201:                 close_transition_token=IF(closed=0 AND VALUES(closed)=1,VALUES(close_transition_token),close_transition_token),
 202:                 updated_at=IF(closed=1,updated_at,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)),
 203:                 closed=GREATEST(closed,VALUES(closed))
 204:             """;
 205:         // FIX-153: backfill inserts missing identities only, so it cannot fabricate a runtime close.
 206:         return local.update("""
 207:             INSERT INTO candle(symbol,interval_code,open_time,close_time,open_price,high_price,low_price,close_price,
 208:                 volume,quote_asset_volume,number_of_trades,taker_buy_base_volume,taker_buy_quote_volume,
 209:                 closed,ingestion_source,close_transition_token,created_at,updated_at)
 210:             VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6))
 211:             """ + duplicateClause,symbol,intervalCode,Timestamp.from(openTime),Timestamp.from(closeTime),openPrice,highPrice,lowPrice,
 212:             closePrice,volume,quoteAssetVolume,numberOfTrades,takerBuyBaseVolume,takerBuyQuoteVolume,closed,provenance,token);
 213:     }

   1: package com.crypto.execution.service;

   3: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   4: import com.crypto.execution.domain.ExecutionOpportunity;

   8: import java.util.ArrayList;
   9: import java.util.Comparator;

  15: 
  16: /**
  17:  * Thread-bound data/persistence override used only by Administration historical replay.
  18:  *
  19:  * The execution algorithms remain the production services. While this scope is active,
  20:  * their signal lookups are resolved exclusively from replay-generated signals as-of the
  21:  * current replay timestamp, and opportunity persistence is redirected to the supplied
  22:  * shadow sink. Production repositories are never read/written by the scoped execution path.
  23:  */
  24: @Component
  25: public class ExecutionReplayScope {
  26: 
  27:     /** FIX-109: Production parity is the default replay contract. Experimental mode is
  28:      * explicit so replay-only candidate rules can never contaminate a parity result. */
  29:     public enum ReplayLogicMode { PRODUCTION_PARITY, EXPERIMENTAL }
  30: 
  31:     private final ThreadLocal<State> state = new ThreadLocal<>();
  32: 
  33:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink) {
  34:         return open(runId, replaySignals, opportunitySink, ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY);
  35:     }
  36: 
  37:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink,
  38:                       ReplayLogicMode logicMode) {
  39:         if (state.get() != null) throw new IllegalStateException("Execution replay scope is already active on this thread");
  40:         List<TradeSignal> immutable = replaySignals == null ? List.of() : replaySignals.stream()
  41:                 .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null)
  42:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt))
  43:                 .toList();
  44:         state.set(new State(runId, immutable, opportunitySink, logicMode == null ? ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY : logicMode));
  45:         return new Scope();
  46:     }
  47: 
  48:     /** FIX-122: run-local executed boundaries, independent of Production ledger/history. */
  49:     public void recordStop(String symbol, long positionId, Instant at, String reason) {
  50:         if (!"STOP_LOSS".equals(reason)) return;
  51:         State s=required();
  52:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary previous=s.stops.get(symbol);
  53:         if (previous==null || !at.isBefore(previous.executedAt()))
  54:             s.stops.put(symbol,new StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary(positionId,at));
  55:     }
  56:     public StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary stopBoundary(String symbol, Instant asOf) {
  57:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary b=required().stops.get(symbol);
  58:         return b!=null && !b.executedAt().isAfter(asOf) ? b : null;
  59:     }
  60:     public Instant referenceInstant() { return required().reference; }
  61:     public boolean fix122Enabled() { return required().fix122Enabled; }
  62:     public void fix122Enabled(boolean value) { required().fix122Enabled=value; }
  63: 
  64:     public boolean active() { return state.get() != null; }
  65:     public long runId() { return required().runId; }
  66:     public ReplayLogicMode logicMode() { return required().logicMode; }
  67:     public boolean experimental() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.EXPERIMENTAL; }
  68:     public boolean productionParity() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY; }
  69: 
  70:     public void reference(Instant reference) { required().reference = reference; }
  71: 
  72:     /** FIX-056: expose the exact latest replay market-price event to the shared execution-price authority. */
  73:     public void referencePrice(String symbol, Instant observedAt, java.math.BigDecimal price) {
  74:         State s = required();
  75:         s.reference = observedAt;
  76:         s.latestPrice = new ReplayPrice(symbol, observedAt, price);
  77:     }
  78: 
  79:     public Optional<ReplayPrice> latestPrice() {
  80:         State s = required();
  81:         return Optional.ofNullable(s.latestPrice);
  82:     }
  83: 
  84:     /** FIX-112A: Replay-local execution ledger keyed to the exact triggering signal. */
  85:     public boolean entryConsumed(Long signalId) {
  86:         return active() && signalId != null && required().consumedSignalIds.contains(signalId);
  87:     }
  88: 
  89:     /** FIX-112A: record only after that exact shadow BUY (initial or add) executes. */
  90:     public void markEntryConsumed(Long signalId) {
  91:         if (signalId != null) required().consumedSignalIds.add(signalId);
  92:     }
  93: 
  94:     /**
  95:      * Adds a freshly generated replay signal to the as-of context stream. Signal generation
  96:      * uses this incrementally so the production confluence/BTC services can resolve only
  97:      * context that had genuinely been generated by the current replay timestamp.
  98:      */
  99:     public void appendSignal(TradeSignal signal) {
 100:         if (signal == null || signal.getGeneratedAt() == null) return;
 101:         State s = required();
 102:         s.signals.add(signal);
 103:         s.signals.sort(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt));
 104:     }
 105: 
 106:     public List<TradeSignal> recent(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, int limit) {
 107:         State s = required();
 108:         Instant ref = reference != null ? reference : s.reference;
 109:         if (ref == null) return List.of();
 110:         return s.signals.stream()
 111:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 112:                 .filter(x -> !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(ref))
 113:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt).reversed())
 114:                 .limit(limit)
 115:                 .toList();
 116:     }
 117: 
 118:     public Optional<TradeSignal> latestAtOrBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
 119:         return recent(symbol, interval, reference, 1).stream().findFirst();
 120:     }
 121: 
 122:     /**
 123:      * FIX-141: Replay-side equivalent of
 124:      * TradeSignalRepository#findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndCandleOpenTimeLessThanEqualAndGeneratedAtLessThanEqualOrderByCandleOpenTimeDesc.
 125:      * Selects by candle open time (close-time ordering), not generatedAt, so Replay cannot diverge
 126:      * from Production on which BTC snapshot it picks. candleOpenTimeBound is the caller's look-ahead
 127:      * bound (reference minus the interval duration); generatedAt must also be at or before reference.
 128:      */
 129:     public Optional<TradeSignal> latestClosedAtOrBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, Instant candleOpenTimeBound) {
 130:         State s = required();
 131:         if (reference == null || candleOpenTimeBound == null) return Optional.empty();
 132:         return s.signals.stream()
 133:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 134:                 .filter(x -> x.getGeneratedAt() != null && !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(reference))
 135:                 .filter(x -> x.getCandleOpenTime() != null && !x.getCandleOpenTime().isAfter(candleOpenTimeBound))
 136:                 .max(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getCandleOpenTime));
 137:     }
 138: 
 139:     public Optional<TradeSignal> previousBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
 140:         State s = required();
 141:         return s.signals.stream()
 142:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 143:                 .filter(x -> x.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(reference))
 144:                 .max(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt));
 145:     }
 146: 
 147:     public Optional<ExecutionOpportunity> currentOpportunity(String symbol, List<String> statuses) {
 148:         ExecutionOpportunity o = required().opportunity;
 149:         if (o == null || !symbol.equals(o.getSymbol())) return Optional.empty();
 150:         if (statuses != null && !statuses.isEmpty() && !statuses.contains(o.getStatus())) return Optional.empty();
 151:         return Optional.of(o);
 152:     }
 153: 
 154:     public ExecutionOpportunity saveOpportunity(ExecutionOpportunity opportunity) {
 155:         State s = required();
 156:         s.opportunity = opportunity;
 157:         if (s.opportunitySink != null) s.opportunitySink.accept(opportunity);
 158:         return opportunity;
 159:     }

 161:     public final class Scope implements AutoCloseable {
 162:         private boolean closed;
 163:         @Override public void close() {
 164:             if (!closed) {
 165:                 state.remove();
 166:                 closed = true;

 514:     private int verifyEventResolution(long runId, String symbol, String interval, List<Candle> candles,
 515:                                       int progressStart, int progressEnd) {
 516:         int resolved = 0;

 524:                 resolved++;

 529:                         "Replaying " + interval + " candle " + (i + 1) + "/" + size, null);
 530:             }
 531:         }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ReplayParityValidator.java:1-120 =====
   1: package com.crypto.regression.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   4: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
   5: 
   6: import java.util.ArrayList;
   7: import java.util.List;
   8: import java.util.Objects;
   9: 
  10: /**
  11:  * FIX-091 / Fix 6: field-level pre-wallet parity comparator.
  12:  * It deliberately compares trading-intelligence state only; wallet execution is outside scope.
  13:  */
  14: @Service
  15: public class ReplayParityValidator {
  16: 
  17:     public Result compare(TradeSignal production, TradeSignal replay) {
  18:         List<String> mismatches = new ArrayList<>();
  19:         if (production == null || replay == null) {
  20:             mismatches.add("Production and Replay signals must both exist");
  21:             return new Result(false, mismatches);
  22:         }
  23: 
  24:         same(mismatches, "detectedRegime", production.getDetectedRegime(), replay.getDetectedRegime());
  25:         same(mismatches, "candidateRegime", production.getCandidateRegime(), replay.getCandidateRegime());
  26:         same(mismatches, "confirmedRegime", production.getConfirmedRegime(), replay.getConfirmedRegime());
  27:         same(mismatches, "selectedStrategy", production.getSelectedStrategy(), replay.getSelectedStrategy());
  28:         same(mismatches, "originalDecision", production.getOriginalDecision(), replay.getOriginalDecision());
  29:         same(mismatches, "finalDecision", production.getDecision(), replay.getDecision());
  30:         same(mismatches, "rawConfidence", production.getRawConfidenceScore(), replay.getRawConfidenceScore());
  31:         same(mismatches, "effectiveConfidence", production.getEffectiveConfidenceScore(), replay.getEffectiveConfidenceScore());
  32:         same(mismatches, "primaryBlockingStage", production.getPrimaryBlockingStage(), replay.getPrimaryBlockingStage());
  33:         same(mismatches, "btcContextStatus", production.getBtcContextStatus(), replay.getBtcContextStatus());
  34:         same(mismatches, "liquidityStatus", production.getLiquidityStatus(), replay.getLiquidityStatus());
  35:         same(mismatches, "entryAuthority", production.getEntryAuthority(), replay.getEntryAuthority());
  36:         same(mismatches, "entryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent",
  37:                 production.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent(), replay.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent());
  38:         return new Result(mismatches.isEmpty(), List.copyOf(mismatches));
  39:     }
  40: 
  41:     private void same(List<String> mismatches, String field, Object expected, Object actual) {
  42:         if (!Objects.equals(expected, actual)) {
  43:             mismatches.add(field + ": Production=" + expected + ", Replay=" + actual);
  44:         }
  45:     }
  46: 
  47:     public record Result(boolean matches, List<String> mismatches) {}
  48: }

  24:     @GetMapping("/proven-trades/{id}/fix127")

  26:         // Include preceding candles for context, not a guessed BUY/signal pairing.
  27:         return rows(jdbc.queryForMap("""
  28:                 SELECT symbol,DATE_SUB(entry_time,INTERVAL 1 HOUR) AS start_time,
  29:                 COALESCE(exit_time,entry_time) AS end_time FROM proven_analyzed_trade WHERE id=?

  34:                 SELECT signal_id,symbol,interval_code,candle_open_time,origin,status,attempts,
  35:                 updated_at,completed_at,paper_position_id,failure_stage,error_message
  36:                 FROM signal_processing_work WHERE symbol=? AND candle_open_time>=? AND candle_open_time<=?
  37:                 ORDER BY candle_open_time,signal_id LIMIT 501
  38:                 """,window.get("symbol"),window.get("start_time"),window.get("end_time"));
  39:         boolean truncated=rows.size()>500;
  40:         return Map.of("scope","PRODUCTION_CURRENT_PROCESSING_STATE","truncated",truncated,
  41:                 "precedingHour",precedingHour,"startTime",window.get("start_time"),"endTime",window.get("end_time"),
  42:                 "rows",truncated ? rows.subList(0,500) : rows);
  43:     }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/inspector/service/TradeInvestmentHistory.java:1-125 =====
   1: package com.crypto.inspector.service;
   2: 
   3: import org.slf4j.Logger;
   4: import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
   5: import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
   6: 
   7: import java.math.BigDecimal;
   8: import java.math.RoundingMode;
   9: import java.sql.Timestamp;
  10: import java.time.Instant;
  11: import java.time.LocalDateTime;
  12: import java.time.ZoneOffset;
  13: import java.util.*;
  14: 
  15: /**
  16:  * FIX-121: read-only historical investment reporting. No wallet or Replay mutation.
  17:  * A position is a flat-to-flat execution sequence, including partial SELLs and adds.
  18:  * Never pair one convenient BUY with a terminal SELL or infer cost from realized P/L.
  19:  * Instances are request-local: cached ledgers must never leak between requests/runs.
  20:  */
  21: public final class TradeInvestmentHistory {
  22:     private static final Logger LOG = LoggerFactory.getLogger(TradeInvestmentHistory.class);
  23:     private final JdbcTemplate jdbc;
  24:     private final Map<String, List<Leg>> ledgers = new HashMap<>();
  25:     private final Map<List<Leg>, List<Position>> resolved = new IdentityHashMap<>();
  26: 
  27:     public TradeInvestmentHistory(JdbcTemplate jdbc) { this.jdbc = jdbc; }
  28: 
  29:     /** Decimal strings preserve all backend precision through JSON and browser rendering. */
  30:     public record Investment(String entryQuantity, String unitEntryPriceUsdt, String totalInvestedUsdt,
  31:                              int buyCount, String status, String explanation) {
  32:         public static Investment unavailable(String reason) {
  33:             return new Investment(null, null, null, 0, "UNAVAILABLE", reason);
  34:         }
  35:     }
  36: 
  37:     public record Leg(long id, String symbol, String side, Instant time, BigDecimal quantity,
  38:                       BigDecimal price, BigDecimal amount, Long signalId) {}
  39:     public record Position(List<Leg> legs, BigDecimal remaining, Investment investment) {
  40:         Leg first() { return legs.get(0); }
  41:         Leg last() { return legs.get(legs.size() - 1); }
  42:     }
  43: 
  44:     public Investment closedWallet(String symbol, long sellId) {
  45:         return report("WALLET", String.valueOf(sellId), unique(partition(wallet(symbol)).stream()
  46:                 .filter(p -> p.last().id() == sellId && "SELL".equals(p.last().side()) && p.remaining().signum() == 0).toList()));
  47:     }
  48: 
  49:     public Investment openWallet(String symbol, Long entrySignalId, BigDecimal remaining) {
  50:         List<Position> matches = partition(wallet(symbol)).stream()
  51:                 .filter(p -> p.remaining().signum() > 0 && entrySignalId != null
  52:                         && entrySignalId.equals(p.first().signalId()))
  53:                 .filter(p -> remaining != null && closeEnough(p.remaining(), remaining, p.legs().size()))
  54:                 .toList();
  55:         return report("WALLET_OPEN", String.valueOf(entrySignalId), unique(matches));
  56:     }
  57: 
  58:     public List<Leg> closedWalletLegs(String symbol, long sellId) {
  59:         return partition(wallet(symbol)).stream().filter(p -> p.last().id() == sellId
  60:                 && p.remaining().signum() == 0).findFirst().map(Position::legs).orElse(List.of());
  61:     }
  62: 
  63:     private List<Position> partition(List<Leg> legs) {
  64:         return resolved.computeIfAbsent(legs, TradeInvestmentHistory::positions);
  65:     }
  66: 
  67:     private List<Leg> wallet(String symbol) {
  68:         if (symbol == null) return List.of();
  69:         return ledgers.computeIfAbsent("W:" + symbol, key -> read("""
  70:                 SELECT id, symbol, side, executed_at AS execution_time, quantity,
  71:                        price_usdt AS execution_price, gross_amount_usdt AS notional_usdt, signal_id
  72:                 FROM wallet_trade WHERE symbol=? AND status='EXECUTED'
  73:                   AND side IN ('BUY','SELL') ORDER BY executed_at,id
  74:                 """, symbol));
  75:     }
  76: 
  77:     /** Run/archive identity is part of the lookup; same-symbol executions in other runs cannot enter. */
  78:     public void enrichReplay(List<Map<String, Object>> trades, Long archiveBatchId) {
  79:         for (Map<String, Object> trade : trades) trade.put("investment", replay(trade, archiveBatchId));
  80:     }
  81: 
  82:     public Investment replay(Map<String, Object> trade, Long archiveBatchId) {
  83:         Object runId = trade.get("test_run_id");
  84:         String symbol = Objects.toString(trade.get("symbol"), "");
  85:         if (runId == null || symbol.isEmpty()) return Investment.unavailable("Replay source identity is missing.");
  86:         String key = "R:" + archiveBatchId + ":" + runId + ":" + symbol;
  87:         List<Leg> legs = ledgers.computeIfAbsent(key, ignored -> archiveBatchId == null
  88:                 ? read("SELECT id,symbol,side,execution_time,quantity,execution_price,notional_usdt FROM wallet_execution_test WHERE test_run_id=? AND symbol=? ORDER BY execution_time,id", runId, symbol)
  89:                 : read("SELECT id,symbol,side,execution_time,quantity,execution_price,notional_usdt FROM wallet_execution_test_archive WHERE archive_batch_id=? AND test_run_id=? AND symbol=? ORDER BY execution_time,id", archiveBatchId, runId, symbol));
  90:         Instant entry = instant(trade.get("entry_time")), exit = instant(trade.get("exit_time"));
  91:         List<Position> matches = partition(legs).stream()
  92:                 .filter(p -> Objects.equals(p.first().time(), entry))
  93:                 .filter(p -> exit == null ? p.remaining().signum() > 0
  94:                         : p.remaining().signum() == 0 && Objects.equals(p.last().time(), exit))
  95:                 .toList();
  96:         return report("REPLAY", runId + ":" + trade.get("id"), unique(matches));
  97:     }
  98: 
  99:     /** Proven records keep their original source; archives and saved execution points are fallback evidence. */
 100:     public void enrichProven(List<Map<String, Object>> trades) {
 101:         for (Map<String, Object> trade : trades) {
 102:             Investment result;
 103:             if (trade.get("source_wallet_sell_trade_id") instanceof Number sell) {
 104:                 result = closedWallet(Objects.toString(trade.get("symbol"), ""), sell.longValue());
 105:                 if (!"AVAILABLE".equals(result.status())) {
 106:                     List<Leg> saved = read("""
 107:                             SELECT wallet_trade_id AS id, ? AS symbol,side,execution_time,quantity,execution_price
 108:                             FROM proven_trade_execution_point WHERE proven_trade_id=? ORDER BY sequence_no,execution_time
 109:                             """, trade.get("symbol"), trade.get("id"));
 110:                     result = report("PROVEN_POINTS", String.valueOf(trade.get("id")), closed(saved, sell.longValue()));
 111:                 }
 112:             } else if (trade.get("source_test_run_id") != null && trade.get("source_trade_id") != null) {
 113:                 Map<String, Object> source = new HashMap<>(trade);
 114:                 source.put("test_run_id", trade.get("source_test_run_id"));
 115:                 source.put("id", trade.get("source_trade_id"));
 116:                 result = replay(source, null);
 117:                 if (!"AVAILABLE".equals(result.status())) {
 118:                     List<Map<String, Object>> archived = jdbc.queryForList("""
 119:                             SELECT archive_batch_id FROM wallet_position_test_archive
 120:                             WHERE test_run_id=? AND id=? ORDER BY archive_batch_id DESC LIMIT 1
 121:                             """, trade.get("source_test_run_id"), trade.get("source_trade_id"));
 122:                     if (!archived.isEmpty()) result = replay(source, ((Number) archived.get(0).get("archive_batch_id")).longValue());
 123:                 }
 124:             } else result = Investment.unavailable("Historical execution source is missing.");
 125:             trade.put("investment", result);

  14: /** FIX-153: local candle facade in OFF; shared reads remain a rollback capability.

  90:         // it is not labelled database execution or commit time. Filtering unchanged.

  96:         }
  97:         return result;
  98:     }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ReplayParityValidator.java:1-60 =====
   1: package com.crypto.regression.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   4: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
   5: 
   6: import java.util.ArrayList;
   7: import java.util.List;
   8: import java.util.Objects;
   9: 
  10: /**
  11:  * FIX-091 / Fix 6: field-level pre-wallet parity comparator.
  12:  * It deliberately compares trading-intelligence state only; wallet execution is outside scope.
  13:  */
  14: @Service
  15: public class ReplayParityValidator {
  16: 
  17:     public Result compare(TradeSignal production, TradeSignal replay) {
  18:         List<String> mismatches = new ArrayList<>();
  19:         if (production == null || replay == null) {
  20:             mismatches.add("Production and Replay signals must both exist");
  21:             return new Result(false, mismatches);
  22:         }
  23: 
  24:         same(mismatches, "detectedRegime", production.getDetectedRegime(), replay.getDetectedRegime());
  25:         same(mismatches, "candidateRegime", production.getCandidateRegime(), replay.getCandidateRegime());
  26:         same(mismatches, "confirmedRegime", production.getConfirmedRegime(), replay.getConfirmedRegime());
  27:         same(mismatches, "selectedStrategy", production.getSelectedStrategy(), replay.getSelectedStrategy());
  28:         same(mismatches, "originalDecision", production.getOriginalDecision(), replay.getOriginalDecision());
  29:         same(mismatches, "finalDecision", production.getDecision(), replay.getDecision());
  30:         same(mismatches, "rawConfidence", production.getRawConfidenceScore(), replay.getRawConfidenceScore());
  31:         same(mismatches, "effectiveConfidence", production.getEffectiveConfidenceScore(), replay.getEffectiveConfidenceScore());
  32:         same(mismatches, "primaryBlockingStage", production.getPrimaryBlockingStage(), replay.getPrimaryBlockingStage());
  33:         same(mismatches, "btcContextStatus", production.getBtcContextStatus(), replay.getBtcContextStatus());
  34:         same(mismatches, "liquidityStatus", production.getLiquidityStatus(), replay.getLiquidityStatus());
  35:         same(mismatches, "entryAuthority", production.getEntryAuthority(), replay.getEntryAuthority());
  36:         same(mismatches, "entryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent",
  37:                 production.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent(), replay.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent());
  38:         return new Result(mismatches.isEmpty(), List.copyOf(mismatches));
  39:     }
  40: 
  41:     private void same(List<String> mismatches, String field, Object expected, Object actual) {
  42:         if (!Objects.equals(expected, actual)) {
  43:             mismatches.add(field + ": Production=" + expected + ", Replay=" + actual);
  44:         }
  45:     }
  46: 
  47:     public record Result(boolean matches, List<String> mismatches) {}
  48: }

 737:      * FIX-069: write every field mapped by the TradeSignal JPA entity into trade_signal_test.
 738:      * Reflection is intentional here: whenever production TradeSignal gains another @Column,
 739:      * replay inherits it automatically instead of drifting behind a hand-maintained test schema.
 740:      */
 741:     private void persistTradeSignalTest(long runId, TradeSignal signal, Long sourceSignalId, String generationError) {
 742:         try {
 743:             java.util.List<String> columns = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 744:             java.util.List<Object> values = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 745:             columns.add("test_run_id"); values.add(runId);
 746:             // FIX-112D / Replay=Production audit lineage: this ID points only to the
 747:             // exact Production signal for the same symbol + interval + candle_open_time.
 748:             // It is additive Replay metadata and is never an execution authority.
 749:             columns.add("source_signal_id"); values.add(sourceSignalId);
 750:             columns.add("replay_generated"); values.add(1);
 751:             columns.add("generation_error"); values.add(generationError);
 752: 
 753:             for (Field field : TradeSignal.class.getDeclaredFields()) {
 754:                 if ("id".equals(field.getName()) || "createdAt".equals(field.getName())) continue;
 755:                 Column column = field.getAnnotation(Column.class);
 756:                 if (column == null) continue;
 757:                 field.setAccessible(true);
 758:                 Object value = field.get(signal);
 759:                 if (value instanceof Enum<?> e) value = e.name();
 760:                 if (value instanceof Instant instant) value = Timestamp.from(instant);
 761:                 String columnName = column.name();
 762:                 if (columnName == null || columnName.isBlank()) columnName = snakeCase(field.getName());
 763:                 columns.add(columnName);
 764:                 values.add(value);
 765:             }
 766: 
 767:             String placeholders = String.join(",", java.util.Collections.nCopies(values.size(), "?"));
 768:             jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO trade_signal_test (" + String.join(",", columns) + ") VALUES (" + placeholders + ")", values.toArray());
 769:         } catch (Exception e) {
 770:             throw new IllegalStateException("Unable to persist production-shaped replay TradeSignal", e);
 771:         }
 772:     }
 773: 
 774:     private String snakeCase(String value) {
 775:         return value.replaceAll("([a-z0-9])([A-Z])", "$1_$2").toLowerCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT);
 776:     }

 778:     private long intervalSeconds(String interval) {
 779:         return switch (interval) {
 780:             case "1m" -> 60L;
 781:             case "5m" -> 300L;
 782:             case "1h" -> 3600L;
 783:             default -> 60L;
 784:         };
 785:     }

 787:     private int intervalOrder(String interval) {
 788:         return switch (interval) {
 789:             case "1h" -> 0;
 790:             case "5m" -> 1;
 791:             default -> 2;
 792:         };
 793:     }

  62:     public ValidationResult validateBuy(TradeSignal executionSignal, int entryQualityScore) {
  63:         ValidationResult structural = validateBaseSignal(executionSignal, true);
  64:         if (!structural.allowed()) return structural;
  65: 
  66:         WalletSettings settings = settings();
  67:         if (settings.isRequireNewBuyTransition()) {
  68:             TradeSignal previous = previousSignal(executionSignal.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
  69:             // FIX-112A: a bullish label alone is not a consumed entry. Only keep
  70:             // BUY_CONTINUATION protection when that exact previous signal has an
  71:             // executed BUY (Production) or executed shadow BUY (Replay).
  72:             if (isFresh(previous, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_MINUTE_TRANSITION_MAX_AGE)
  73:                     && isBullish(previous.getDecision())
  74:                     && entryConsumptionPolicy.resolve(previous.getId()) != EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED) {
  75:                 return ValidationResult.reject("BUY_CONTINUATION",
  76:                         "BUY was not executed because a recent bullish opportunity for this symbol was already consumed. "
  77:                                 + "A new WATCH/NEUTRAL/SELL -> BUY transition is required.");
  78:             }
  79:         }
  80: 
  81:         TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
  82:         if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
  83:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
  84:                     "No fresh 5m confirmation was available for the 1m BUY signal.");
  85:         }
  86: 
  87:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
  88:         if (!isFresh(oneHour, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
  89:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT",
  90:                     "No fresh 1h strategic context was available for the 1m BUY signal.");
  91:         }
  92: 
  93:         HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment=htfAssessment.assess(fiveMinute,true,oneHour,true);
  94:         if(!assessment.allowed()) {
  95:             recordAssessmentMetric(assessment);
  96:             audit(executionSignal, "DIRECT_BUY", assessment, assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY.");
  97:             return ValidationResult.reject(assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY.");
  98:         }
  99: 
 100:         ExecutionProfile profile = ExecutionProfile.from(settings.getExecutionProfile());
 101:         ValidationResult profileResult = switch (profile) {
 102:             case CONSERVATIVE -> conservativeBuy(assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 103:             // FIX-112B: only the fresh direct-BUY BALANCED path receives the narrow
 104:             // 5m-NEUTRAL exception. validateBuyContext() and AGGRESSIVE fallback keep

 108:             case AGGRESSIVE -> aggressiveBuy(executionSignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 109:         };
 110:         ValidationResult result = cap(profileResult, assessment);
 111:         audit(executionSignal, "DIRECT_BUY", assessment, result.code(), result.explanation());
 112:         return result;
 113:     }
