===== src/main/java/com/crypto/wallet/service/BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.java:1-125 =====
   1: package com.crypto.wallet.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.client.binance.BinanceMarketDataClient;
   4: import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
   5: import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
   6: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   7: 
   8: import java.math.BigDecimal;
   9: import java.math.MathContext;
  10: import java.util.Locale;
  11: 
  12: /**
  13:  * FIX-11T minimum-executable-order guard shared by Production Paper and Replay.
  14:  *
  15:  * The project intentionally models Paper/Replay as real Binance trading with only the
  16:  * final connector missing.  Therefore a Near-TP partial harvest that Binance would reject
  17:  * for being below the symbol's minimum notional is not simulated.  No alternative exit
  18:  * size is invented: the position simply continues through its existing management logic.
  19:  *
  20:  * This class deliberately checks only the agreed minimum executable notional boundary.
  21:  * It does not add a new Near-TP-specific step-size/precision model.
  22:  */
  23: @Component
  24: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  25: @Slf4j
  26: public class BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy {
  27:     private static final MathContext MC = MathContext.DECIMAL64;
  28: 
  29:     private final BinanceMarketDataClient binanceMarketDataClient;
  30:     public Evaluation evaluate(String symbol, BigDecimal quantity, BigDecimal price) {
  31:         if (symbol == null || symbol.isBlank() || quantity == null || quantity.signum() <= 0
  32:                 || price == null || price.signum() <= 0) {
  33:             return new Evaluation(false, "INVALID_ORDER_INPUT", BigDecimal.ZERO, null);
  34:         }
  35:         String pair = symbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
  36:         BigDecimal notional = quantity.multiply(price, MC);
  37:         BigDecimal minimum;
  38:         try {
  39:             minimum = binanceMarketDataClient.getMinimumExecutableNotional(pair);
  40:         } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
  41:             // Fail safe. Missing exchange constraints must never be converted into permission
  42:             // to create an order that real Binance might reject.
  43:             log.warn("FIX-11T Binance minimum executable amount unavailable: symbol={}, requestedNotional={}, error={}",
  44:                     pair, notional, ex.getMessage());
  45:             return new Evaluation(false, "BINANCE_MINIMUM_UNAVAILABLE", notional, null);
  46:         }
  47:         if (notional.compareTo(minimum) < 0) {
  48:             return new Evaluation(false, "BELOW_BINANCE_MINIMUM", notional, minimum);
  49:         }
  50:         return new Evaluation(true, "EXECUTABLE", notional, minimum);
  51:     }
  52: 
  53:     public record Evaluation(boolean executable, String code, BigDecimal requestedNotional, BigDecimal minimumNotional) {
  54:     }
  55: }

 111:     /**
 112:      * Historical/as-of sentiment view. No sentiment row newer than evaluatedAt
 113:      * is visible to the caller. This keeps Administration replay deterministic
 114:      * and prevents current/future sentiment from leaking into historical runs.
 115:      */
 116:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
 117:     public SentimentOverview overviewAsOf(String symbol, Instant evaluatedAt) {
 118:         String normalized = normalizeSymbol(symbol);
 119:         Instant reference = evaluatedAt == null ? Instant.now() : evaluatedAt;
 120:         if (!properties.enabled()) {
 121:             return new SentimentOverview(
 122:                     normalized,
 123:                     BigDecimal.ZERO,
 124:                     "DISABLED",
 125:                     0,
 126:                     List.of(),
 127:                     List.of()
 128:             );
 129:         }
 130:         List<SentimentSignal> recent = repository
 131:                 .findTop20BySymbolAndObservedAtLessThanEqualOrderByObservedAtDesc(normalized, reference);
 132:         Aggregation aggregation = aggregate(normalized, reference);
 133:         return new SentimentOverview(
 134:                 normalized,
 135:                 aggregation.score(),
 136:                 label(aggregation.score()),
 137:                 aggregation.sampleCount(),
 138:                 aggregation.providers(),
 139:                 recent
 140:         );

 167:     private Aggregation aggregate(String symbol, Instant reference) {
 168:         Instant now = reference == null ? Instant.now() : reference;
 169:         Instant after = now.minus(properties.activeWindow());
 170:         List<SentimentSignal> signals = repository
 171:                 .findBySymbolAndObservedAtAfterAndObservedAtLessThanEqualOrderByObservedAtDesc(
 172:                         symbol, after, now);
 173: 
 174:         Map<String, List<SentimentSignal>> byProvider = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 175:         for (SentimentSignal signal : signals) {
 176:             String provider = SentimentProviderName.normalize(signal.getSource());
 177:             byProvider.computeIfAbsent(provider, ignored -> new ArrayList<>()).add(signal);
 178:         }
 179: 
 180:         for (com.crypto.domain.SentimentProviderConfig configured : providerConfigService.findAll()) {
 181:             byProvider.computeIfAbsent(configured.getProviderCode(), ignored -> new ArrayList<>());
 182:         }
 183: 
 184:         List<ProviderSentiment> breakdown = new ArrayList<>();
 185:         BigDecimal total = BigDecimal.ZERO;
 186:         BigDecimal denominator = BigDecimal.ZERO;
 187: 
 188:         for (Map.Entry<String, List<SentimentSignal>> entry : byProvider.entrySet()) {
 189:             String providerName = entry.getKey();
 190:             com.crypto.domain.SentimentProviderConfig providerConfig = providerConfigService.require(providerName);
 191:             ProviderAggregate provider = aggregateProvider(entry.getValue(), now);
 192:             BigDecimal configuredWeight = providerConfig.getWeight();
 193:             BigDecimal effectiveWeight = provider.confidence().multiply(configuredWeight, MC);
 194: 
 195:             boolean historicalReference = now.isBefore(Instant.now().minusSeconds(1));
 196:             boolean healthyEnough = historicalReference
 197:                     ? provider.sampleCount() > 0
 198:                     : providerConfigService.contributes(providerConfig, now);
 199:             if (properties.enabled() && providerConfig.isEnabled() && healthyEnough
 200:                     && provider.sampleCount() > 0 && effectiveWeight.signum() > 0) {
 201:                 total = total.add(provider.score().multiply(effectiveWeight, MC), MC);
 202:                 denominator = denominator.add(effectiveWeight, MC);
 203:             }
 204: 
 205:             breakdown.add(new ProviderSentiment(
 206:                     providerName,
 207:                     properties.enabled() && providerConfig.isEnabled(),
 208:                     configuredWeight,
 209:                     provider.score(),
 210:                     provider.confidence(),
 211:                     properties.enabled() && providerConfig.isEnabled() && healthyEnough
 212:                             ? effectiveWeight : BigDecimal.ZERO,
 213:                     provider.sampleCount(),
 214:                     provider.latestObservedAt()
 215:             ));
 216:         }
 217: 
 218:         breakdown.sort(Comparator
 219:                 .comparing(ProviderSentiment::effectiveWeight).reversed()
 220:                 .thenComparing(ProviderSentiment::provider));
===== src/main/java/com/crypto/service/RegimeStateService.java:1-130 =====
   1: package com.crypto.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.MarketRegime;
   4: import com.crypto.dto.MarketRegimeAssessment;
   5: import com.crypto.execution.service.ExecutionReplayScope;
   6: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
   7: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
   8: 
   9: import java.time.Instant;
  10: import java.util.HashMap;
  11: import java.util.List;
  12: import java.util.Map;
  13: 
  14: /**
  15:  * FIX-091 / Fix 4 + FIX-109: Experimental-Replay-only regime persistence state machine.
  16:  * Production behavior is intentionally untouched until the parity gate is passed.
  17:  * State is keyed by replay run + symbol + interval and advances only on candle timestamps.
  18:  */
  19: @Service
  20: public class RegimeStateService {
  21:     private final ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;
  22:     private final int requiredCandles;
  23:     // Run id is part of the key so concurrent Replay/Test workers cannot reset or contaminate
  24:     // one another. The map contains only Replay state; Production never reads it.
  25:     private final Map<String, RegimeState> replayState = new HashMap<>();
  26: 
  27:     public RegimeStateService(ExecutionReplayScope replayScope,
  28:                               @Value("${analysis.regime.persistence-candles:3}") int requiredCandles) {
  29:         this.replayScope = replayScope;
  30:         this.requiredCandles = Math.max(2, requiredCandles);
  31:     }
  32: 
  33:     public synchronized Decision apply(String symbol, String interval, Instant candleTime,
  34:                                        MarketRegimeAssessment detectedAssessment) {
  35:         if (detectedAssessment == null || !replayScope.active() || !replayScope.experimental()) {
  36:             MarketRegime regime = detectedAssessment == null ? MarketRegime.UNKNOWN : detectedAssessment.regime();
  37:             return new Decision(detectedAssessment, regime, null, regime, 0, false);
  38:         }
  39: 
  40:         long runId = replayScope.runId();
  41:         String key = runId + "|" + symbol + "|" + interval;
  42:         RegimeState state = replayState.get(key);
  43:         MarketRegime detected = detectedAssessment.regime();
  44:         if (state == null) {
  45:             state = new RegimeState(detected, null, 0, candleTime);
  46:             replayState.put(key, state);
  47:             return new Decision(detectedAssessment, detected, null, detected, 0, false);
  48:         }
  49: 
  50:         MarketRegime confirmed = state.confirmed;
  51:         MarketRegime candidate = state.candidate;
  52:         int count = state.candidateCount;
  53:         boolean promoted = false;
  54: 
  55:         if (detected == confirmed) {
  56:             candidate = null;
  57:             count = 0;
  58:         } else if (detected == candidate) {
  59:             count++;
  60:         } else {
  61:             candidate = detected;
  62:             count = 1;
  63:         }
  64: 
  65:         if (candidate != null && count >= requiredCandles) {
  66:             confirmed = candidate;
  67:             candidate = null;
  68:             count = 0;
  69:             promoted = true;
  70:         }
  71:         replayState.put(key, new RegimeState(confirmed, candidate, count, candleTime));
  72: 
  73:         MarketRegimeAssessment effective = detected == confirmed
  74:                 ? detectedAssessment
  75:                 : new MarketRegimeAssessment(confirmed, detectedAssessment.confidence(),
  76:                     append(detectedAssessment.evidence(), "FIX-091 Replay persistence: detected=" + detected
  77:                             + ", candidate=" + candidate + ", confirmed=" + confirmed
  78:                             + ", candidateCount=" + count + "/" + requiredCandles + "."));
  79:         return new Decision(effective, detected, candidate, confirmed, count, promoted);
  80:     }
  81: 
  82:     private List<String> append(List<String> evidence, String value) {
  83:         java.util.ArrayList<String> result = new java.util.ArrayList<>(evidence == null ? List.of() : evidence);
  84:         result.add(value);
  85:         return List.copyOf(result);
  86:     }
  87: 
  88:     private record RegimeState(MarketRegime confirmed, MarketRegime candidate, int candidateCount, Instant lastCandleTime) {}
  89: 
  90:     public record Decision(MarketRegimeAssessment effectiveAssessment,
  91:                            MarketRegime detectedRegime,
  92:                            MarketRegime candidateRegime,
  93:                            MarketRegime confirmedRegime,
  94:                            int candidateCount,
  95:                            boolean promoted) {}
  96: }
===== src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/DynamicProfitLockService.java:1-115 =====
   1: package com.crypto.position.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;

  13: import java.math.BigDecimal;
  14: import java.math.RoundingMode;
  15: import java.time.Instant;

  18: /**
  19:  * Position-manager profit protection for already-open long positions.
  20:  *
  21:  * The BUY/SELL analysis engine is deliberately not involved here. The lock is
  22:  * derived only from the immutable entry price/take-profit stored on the wallet
  23:  * position and the best price observed after entry.
  24:  *
  25:  * Adaptive policy:
  26:  *  - derive an entry-quality score from the immutable entry signal score and confidence;
  27:  *  - stronger setups receive more room before protection activates;
  28:  *  - weaker/medium setups protect profits earlier;
  29:  *  - the protected price can only move upward and never loosens after activation.
  30:  *
  31:  * Administration values remain the fallback when entry-quality information is unavailable.
  32:  */
  33: @Service
  34: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  35: @Slf4j
  36: public class DynamicProfitLockService {
  37: 
  38:     private static final BigDecimal HUNDRED = BigDecimal.valueOf(100);
  39:     private static final int SCALE = 12;
  40: 
  41:     private final WalletManagedPositionRepository positionRepository;
  42:     private final WalletSettingsRepository settingsRepository;
  43:     private final ProfitLockPolicy profitLockPolicy;
  44: 
  45:     @Transactional
  46:     public Evaluation evaluate(TradeSignal signal) {
  47:         if (signal == null) {
  48:             return Evaluation.inactive("No valid market price is available.");
  49:         }
  50:         return evaluatePrice(signal.getSymbol(), signal.getLatestPrice());
  51:     }
  52: 
  53:     /**
  54:      * Evaluates Dynamic Profit Lock directly from a live market price.
  55:      * This path is used by live position protection between candle-close signals.
  56:      *
  57:      * FIX-118 behavioral phase:
  58:      * TP_EXTENSION_REBASE preserves the historically earned lock in persistence but makes
  59:      * it non-executable until progress against the CURRENT TP reaches the configured
  60:      * activation threshold again. This gives an approved continuation meaningful room
  61:      * without forgetting that profit protection had already been earned.
  62:      */
  63:     @Transactional
  64:     public Evaluation evaluatePrice(String symbolValue, BigDecimal currentPrice) {
  65:         if (symbolValue == null || symbolValue.isBlank() || currentPrice == null
  66:                 || currentPrice.signum() <= 0) {
  67:             return Evaluation.inactive("No valid market price is available.");
  68:         }
  69: 
  70:         String symbol = symbolValue.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
  71:         WalletManagedPosition position = positionRepository
  72:                 .findFirstBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(symbol, "OPEN")
  73:                 .orElse(null);
  74:         if (position == null) {
  75:             return Evaluation.inactive("No open wallet position exists.");
  76:         }
  77: 
  78:         WalletSettings settings = settings();
  79:         if (!settings.isDynamicProfitLockEnabled()) {
  80:             return Evaluation.inactive("Dynamic Profit Lock is disabled in Administration.");
  81:         }
  82: 
  83:         BigDecimal entry = position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt();
  84:         BigDecimal target = position.getTakeProfitUsdt();
  85:         BigDecimal current = currentPrice;
  86:         if (entry == null || target == null || entry.signum() <= 0 || target.compareTo(entry) <= 0) {
  87:             return Evaluation.inactive("The position does not have a valid long take-profit distance.");
  88:         }
  89: 
  90:         ProfitLockProfile profile = configuredProfile(settings);
  91:         BigDecimal activation = profile.activationPercent();
  92:         BigDecimal initialLock = profile.initialLockPercent();
  93:         BigDecimal trailStep = profile.trailStepPercent();
  94:         BigDecimal previousHighest = position.getHighestPriceUsdt();
  95:         boolean previousPersistedActive = position.isProfitLockActive();
  96:         BigDecimal previousLock = position.getProfitLockPriceUsdt();
  97:         BigDecimal previousProgress = position.getProfitLockProgressPercent();
  98:         ProfitLockState previousState = normalizedState(position);
  99: 
 100:         BigDecimal highest = previousHighest == null ? current : previousHighest.max(current);
 101:         BigDecimal progress = progressPercent(entry, target, highest);
 102:         BigDecimal lockPrice = previousLock;
 103:         ProfitLockState state = previousState;
 104:         boolean executableActive;
 105:         boolean triggered;
 106: 
 107:         if (previousState == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE && progress.compareTo(activation) < 0) {
 108:             // Keep the legacy active flag/lock persisted so history is not lost, but do not
 109:             // allow the superseded geometry to trigger an exit during the rebase window.
 110:             executableActive = false;
 111:             triggered = false;
 112:         } else {
 113:             // FIX-118 refinement: when a TP-extension rebase earns activation against the
 114:             // CURRENT target, rebuild the executable lock from the current geometry instead
 115:             // of carrying the historical pre-extension lock through ProfitLockPolicy's
===== src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.java:1-105 =====

  16:  * <p>TradeSignal.latestPrice is immutable decision-time evidence and must never be
  17:  * treated as an execution fill. Production resolves the newest canonical Binance 1m
  18:  * live-price event recorded by FIX-052. Administration Replay resolves the newest
  19:  * replay price event already consumed by ExecutionReplayScope. This gives both paths
  20:  * one price authority while preserving the original signal snapshot for audit.</p>
  21:  */
  22: @Service
  23: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  24: public class ExecutionPriceAuthorityService {
  25:     private static final Duration MAX_EXECUTION_PRICE_AGE = Duration.ofSeconds(15);
  26: 
  27:     private final MarketPriceEventService marketPriceEventService;
  28:     private final ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;
  29: 
  30:     public Optional<ExecutionPrice> resolve(String symbol, Instant referenceTime) {
  31:         // FIX-109 / Replay parity: wall-clock fallback is forbidden. Every caller must
  32:         // supply the evaluation clock explicitly so historical Replay can never silently
  33:         // compare an old market event with the machine clock used by Production today.
  34:         Instant reference = Objects.requireNonNull(referenceTime,
  35:                 "Execution price reference time is required");
  36: 
  37:         if (replayScope.active()) {
  38:             return replayScope.latestPrice()
  39:                     .filter(p -> symbol != null && symbol.equalsIgnoreCase(p.symbol()))
  40:                     .filter(p -> !p.observedAt().isAfter(reference))
  41:                     .filter(p -> !Duration.between(p.observedAt(), reference).isNegative())
  42:                     .filter(p -> Duration.between(p.observedAt(), reference).compareTo(MAX_EXECUTION_PRICE_AGE) <= 0)
  43:                     .map(p -> new ExecutionPrice(p.price(), p.observedAt(), "REPLAY_MARKET_PRICE_EVENT"));
  44:         }
  45: 
  46:         return marketPriceEventService.findLatestAtOrBefore(symbol, reference)
  47:                 .filter(p -> Duration.between(p.observedAt(), reference).compareTo(MAX_EXECUTION_PRICE_AGE) <= 0)
  48:                 .map(p -> new ExecutionPrice(p.price(), p.observedAt(), p.source()));
  49:     }
  50: 
  51:     public record ExecutionPrice(BigDecimal price, Instant observedAt, String source) {}
  52: }

 252:                                           TradeSignal oneMinute, TradeSignal fiveMinute) {
 253:         // FIX-11T evidence is explicitly time-safe. Normal Production logic above keeps its

 263:                 managed.getNearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(), managed.isNearTpHarvestUsed());

  25: /**
  26:  * FIX-11Q ƒ?" Replay-only one-candle continuation grace experiment.
  27:  *
  28:  * The Production-parity Shadow position always remains authoritative. This observer starts a
  29:  * separate counterfactual position only after the existing PositionContinuationPolicy has already
  30:  * returned FAIL at a reached take-profit checkpoint. Existing continuation PASS paths therefore
  31:  * remain untouched and continue to extend take profit exactly as before.
  32:  *
  33:  * The counterfactual holds through one specific NEUTRAL 1m candle without moving TP. The grace is
  34:  * tied to that candle (not one live-price tick), may be consumed only once per position lifetime,
  35:  * and normal shared continuation/exit policies resume after a new 1m candle arrives.
  36:  */
  37: @Slf4j
  38: @Service
  39: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  40: public class OneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver {
  41:     private static final MathContext MC = MathContext.DECIMAL64;
  42: 
  43:     private final JdbcTemplate jdbcTemplate;
  44:     private final PositionContinuationPolicy continuationPolicy;
  45:     private final PositionExitPolicy exitPolicy;
  46:     private final ProfitLockPolicy profitLockPolicy;
  47:     private final TradeExecutionValidationService executionValidationService;
  48:     private final WalletSettingsRepository walletSettingsRepository;
  49:

  51:     private final Map<Long, java.util.Set<Long>> consumedByRun = new HashMap<>();

  53:     public synchronized void startRun(long runId, String symbol) {
  54:         activeByRun.remove(runId);
  55:         consumedByRun.put(runId, new java.util.HashSet<>());
  56:         log.info("FIX11Q_GRACE_START runId={} symbol={} mode=REPLAY_COUNTERFACTUAL productionMutation=false baselineReplayMutation=false", runId, symbol);
  57:     }

  59:     public synchronized void observeBaselineTakeProfitFailure(
  60:             long runId, String symbol, long positionId, Instant observedAt, BigDecimal price,
  61:             Instant entryTime, BigDecimal entryPrice, BigDecimal quantity, BigDecimal cost,
  62:             BigDecimal stopLoss, BigDecimal takeProfit, BigDecimal highest,
  63:             boolean profitLockActive, BigDecimal profitLockPrice,
  64:             int entryScore, int entryConfidence, int entryTrend, int entryStructure,
  65:             int entryMomentum, int entryVolume,
  66:             TradeSignal current1m, TradeSignal fiveMinute, TradeSignal oneHour,
  67:             String baselineExplanation) {
  68: 
  69:         if (activeByRun.containsKey(runId) || consumedByRun.getOrDefault(runId, java.util.Set.of()).contains(positionId)) return;
  70:         if (!eligible(current1m, fiveMinute, oneHour, entryMomentum)) return;
  71: 
  72:         Instant candleIdentity = candleIdentity(current1m);
  73:         if (candleIdentity == null) return;
  74:         BigDecimal baselinePnl = price.multiply(quantity, MC).subtract(cost, MC);
  75:         BigDecimal baselinePnlPct = percentage(entryPrice, price);
  76:         BigDecimal safeHighest = highest == null ? price : highest.max(price);
  77: 
  78:         Variant v = new Variant(positionId, symbol, entryTime, entryPrice, quantity, cost, stopLoss,
  79:                 takeProfit, safeHighest, profitLockActive, profitLockPrice,
  80:                 entryScore, entryConfidence, entryTrend, entryStructure, entryMomentum, entryVolume,
  81:                 candleIdentity, current1m == null ? null : current1m.getId(), observedAt, price,
  82:                 baselinePnl, baselinePnlPct, price, price, current1m, fiveMinute, oneHour);
  83:         activeByRun.put(runId, v);
  84:         consumedByRun.computeIfAbsent(runId, k -> new java.util.HashSet<>()).add(positionId);
  85: 
  86:         jdbcTemplate.update("""
  87:                 INSERT INTO one_candle_continuation_grace_test

  94:                 """, runId, positionId, symbol, Timestamp.from(observedAt), v.graceSignalId(), Timestamp.from(candleIdentity),
  95:                 price, entryPrice, takeProfit, takeProfit, name(current1m.getDecision()), name(fiveMinute.getDecision()),
  96:                 name(oneHour.getDecision()), current1m.getTrendScore(), trendFloor(entryTrend),

 106:     public synchronized void onSignal(long runId, TradeSignal signal, TradeSignal latest1m, TradeSignal latest5m, TradeSignal latest1h) {
 107:         Variant v = activeByRun.get(runId);
 108:         if (v == null || signal == null) return;
 109:         if ("1m".equals(signal.getInterval()) && v.next1mDecision() == null && !sameGraceCandle(v, signal)) {
 110:             v = v.withNext1m(name(signal.getDecision()));
 111:         }
 112:         if ("5m".equals(signal.getInterval()) && v.next5mDecision() == null && signal.getGeneratedAt() != null && signal.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(v.graceAt())) {
 113:             v = v.withNext5m(name(signal.getDecision()));
 114:         }
 115:         if ("1h".equals(signal.getInterval()) && v.next1hDecision() == null && signal.getGeneratedAt() != null && signal.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(v.graceAt())) {
 116:             v = v.withNext1h(name(signal.getDecision()));
 117:         }
 118:         activeByRun.put(runId, v.withContext(latest1m, latest5m, latest1h));
 119:     }

 121:     public synchronized void onPrice(long runId, String symbol, MarketPriceEventService.PriceEvent event,
 122:                                      TradeSignal latest1m, TradeSignal latest5m, TradeSignal latest1h) {
 123:         Variant v = activeByRun.get(runId);
 124:         if (v == null || event == null || event.price() == null || !v.symbol().equals(symbol)) return;
 125:         if (!event.observedAt().isAfter(v.graceAt())) return;
 126: 
 127:         BigDecimal price = event.price();
 128:         v = v.withContext(latest1m, latest5m, latest1h).withPath(price);
 129:         boolean sameGraceCandle = sameGraceCandle(v, latest1m);
 130: 
 131:         // Hard protection remains active even during grace; FIX-11Q suppresses only the baseline
 132:         // TAKE_PROFIT exit caused by transient continuation failure.
 133:         if (v.stopLoss() != null && price.compareTo(v.stopLoss()) <= 0) {
 134:             close(runId, v, event.observedAt(), price, "STOP_LOSS", "Stop loss reached during/after grace."); return;
 135:         }
 136: 
 137:         v = updateProfitLock(v, price);
 138:         if (v.profitLockActive() && v.profitLockPrice() != null && price.compareTo(v.profitLockPrice()) <= 0) {
 139:             BigDecimal hardFloor = v.entryPrice().multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(1.0005), MC);
 140:             if (price.compareTo(hardFloor) < 0) {
 141:                 close(runId, v, event.observedAt(), price, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", "Profit-lock hard floor breached in FIX-11Q counterfactual."); return;
 142:             }
 143:             PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation lock = exitPolicy.evaluateProfitLockBreach(latest1m, latest5m, latest1h);
 144:             if (lock.exit()) { close(runId, v, event.observedAt(), price, lock.code(), lock.explanation()); return; }
 145:         }
 146: 
 147:         if (v.takeProfit() != null && price.compareTo(v.takeProfit()) >= 0) {
 148:             if (sameGraceCandle) {
 149:                 activeByRun.put(runId, v); // hold entire candle; target intentionally unchanged
 150:                 return;
 151:             }
 152:             PositionContinuationPolicy.Evaluation continuation = continuationPolicy.evaluate(
 153:                     latest1m, latest5m, latest1h, v.entryTrend(), v.entryStructure(), v.entryMomentum(), v.entryVolume(),
 154:                     v.entryConfidence(), v.entryScore());
 155:             if (continuation.extendTarget()) {
 156:                 BigDecimal distance = v.takeProfit().subtract(v.entryPrice());
 157:                 BigDecimal newTarget = v.takeProfit().add(distance.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.50), MC), MC);
 158:                 activeByRun.put(runId, v.withTakeProfit(newTarget));
 159:                 log.info("FIX11Q_VARIANT_TP_EXTENDED runId={} positionId={} oldTp={} newTp={} reason={}", runId, v.positionId(), v.takeProfit(), newTarget, continuation.explanation());
 160:                 return;
 161:             }
 162:             close(runId, v, event.observedAt(), price, "TAKE_PROFIT", continuation.explanation()); return;
 163:         }
 164: 
 165:         if (!sameGraceCandle) {
 166:             PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation normal = exitPolicy.evaluateNormalExit(latest1m, latest5m, latest1h);
 167:             if (normal.exit()) { close(runId, v, event.observedAt(), price, normal.code(), normal.explanation()); return; }
 168:             if (latest1m != null && "1m".equals(latest1m.getInterval()) && bearish(latest1m.getDecision())) {
 169:                 TradeExecutionValidationService.ValidationResult validated = executionValidationService.validateSell(latest1m);
 170:                 if (validated.allowed()) { close(runId, v, event.observedAt(), price, validated.code(), validated.explanation()); return; }
 171:             }
 172:         }
 173:         activeByRun.put(runId, v);
 174:     }

 176:     public synchronized void finishRun(long runId, String symbol, Instant endTime) {
 177:         Variant v = activeByRun.remove(runId);
 178:         if (v != null) {
 179:             BigDecimal pnl = v.lastPrice().multiply(v.quantity(), MC).subtract(v.cost(), MC);
 180:             updateRow(runId, v, endTime, v.lastPrice(), "OPEN_AT_WINDOW_END", pnl, percentage(v.entryPrice(), v.lastPrice()));
 181:         }
 182:         Integer count = jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM one_candle_continuation_grace_test WHERE test_run_id=?", Integer.class, runId);
 183:         log.info("FIX11Q_GRACE_SUMMARY runId={} symbol={} candidates={} mode=REPLAY_COUNTERFACTUAL", runId, symbol, count == null ? 0 : count);
 184:         consumedByRun.remove(runId);
 185:     }

 187:     private boolean eligible(TradeSignal one, TradeSignal five, TradeSignal hour, int entryMomentum) {
 188:         return one != null && five != null && hour != null
 189:                 && one.getDecision() == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL
 190:                 && bullish(hour.getDecision()) && !bearish(five.getDecision())
 191:                 && one.getMomentumScore() >= momentumFloor(entryMomentum);
 192:     }

 194:     private Variant updateProfitLock(Variant v, BigDecimal price) {
 195:         WalletSettings s = walletSettingsRepository.findById(1L).orElseGet(() -> WalletSettings.builder()
 196:                 .id(1L).dynamicProfitLockEnabled(true).profitLockActivationPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(70))
 197:                 .profitLockInitialPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(40)).profitLockTrailStepPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(10)).build());
 198:         ProfitLockPolicy.State st = profitLockPolicy.evaluate(v.entryPrice(), v.takeProfit(), price, v.highest(),
 199:                 v.profitLockActive(), v.profitLockPrice(), s.isDynamicProfitLockEnabled(),
 200:                 nvl(s.getProfitLockActivationPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(70)),
 201:                 nvl(s.getProfitLockInitialPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(40)),
 202:                 nvl(s.getProfitLockTrailStepPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(10)));
 203:         return v.withLock(st.highestPrice(), st.active(), st.lockPrice());
 204:     }

 206:     private void close(long runId, Variant v, Instant at, BigDecimal price, String reason, String explanation) {
 207:         BigDecimal pnl = price.multiply(v.quantity(), MC).subtract(v.cost(), MC);
 208:         updateRow(runId, v, at, price, reason, pnl, percentage(v.entryPrice(), price));
 209:         activeByRun.remove(runId);
 210:         log.info("FIX11Q_VARIANT_CLOSED runId={} positionId={} symbol={} at={} price={} reason={} baselinePnl={} variantPnl={} delta={}",
 211:                 runId, v.positionId(), v.symbol(), at, price, reason, v.baselinePnl(), pnl, pnl.subtract(v.baselinePnl(), MC));
 212:     }

 214:     private void updateRow(long runId, Variant v, Instant at, BigDecimal price, String reason, BigDecimal pnl, BigDecimal pnlPct) {
 215:         String result = result(v);

 217:                 UPDATE one_candle_continuation_grace_test SET

 221:                 WHERE test_run_id=? AND position_test_id=?
 222:                 """, v.next1mDecision(), v.next5mDecision(), v.next1hDecision(), Timestamp.from(at), price,
 223:                 reason, pnl, pnlPct, pnl.subtract(v.baselinePnl(), MC), percentage(v.priceAtGrace(), v.maxPrice()),
 224:                 percentage(v.priceAtGrace(), v.minPrice()), result, reason.equals("OPEN_AT_WINDOW_END") ? reason : "CLOSED",
 225:                 runId, v.positionId());

2534:     private void saveOpportunity(TradeSignal signal, Evidence evidence, String status, String source,
2535:                                  int positionPercent, String code, String explanation) {
2536:         Instant now = Instant.now();
2537:         ExecutionOpportunity opportunity = currentOpportunity(signal.getSymbol(), List.of("BUILDING", "WEAKENING", "BLOCKED", "CONFIRMED"))
2538:                 .orElseGet(() -> ExecutionOpportunity.builder()
2539:                         .symbol(signal.getSymbol())
2540:                         .direction("BUY")
2541:                         .startedAt(signal.getGeneratedAt())
2542:                         .createdAt(now)
2543:                         .build());
2544: 
2545:         opportunity.setStatus(normalizeStatus(status));
2546:         opportunity.setLastEvidenceAt(signal.getGeneratedAt());
2547:         opportunity.setLatestSignal(signal);
2548: 
2549:         // FIX-055: keep the opportunity's actual price history independent of the
2550:         // recent-signal lookup window. For a BUY opportunity, the first valid price
2551:         // is the anchor and the lowest valid observed price is the best entry reference.
2552:         if (signal.getLatestPrice() != null && signal.getLatestPrice().signum() > 0) {
2553:             if (opportunity.getAnchorEntryPrice() == null || opportunity.getAnchorEntryPrice().signum() <= 0) {
2554:                 opportunity.setAnchorEntryPrice(signal.getLatestPrice());
2555:             }
2556:             if (opportunity.getBestEntryPrice() == null || opportunity.getBestEntryPrice().signum() <= 0
2557:                     || signal.getLatestPrice().compareTo(opportunity.getBestEntryPrice()) < 0) {
2558:                 opportunity.setBestEntryPrice(signal.getLatestPrice());
2559:             }
2560:         }
2561:         // FIX-091 / Fix 8: update or reset the opportunity peak using the current signal.
2562:         // The peak expires after 15 minutes so an old BUY cannot permanently poison a newly
2563:         // rebuilt setup. A genuinely stronger current signal establishes a fresh peak.
2564:         updateOpportunityPeak(opportunity, signal);
2565: 
2566:         opportunity.setEvidenceCount(evidence.observationCount());
2567:         opportunity.setBuyCount(evidence.buyCount());
2568:         opportunity.setWatchCount(evidence.watchCount());
2569:         opportunity.setNeutralCount(evidence.neutralCount());
2570:         opportunity.setBearishCount(evidence.bearishCount());
2571:         opportunity.setEvidenceScore(evidence.evidenceScore());
2572:         opportunity.setOpportunityHealth(evidence.opportunityHealth());
2573:         opportunity.setHealthMomentum(evidence.healthMomentum());
2574:         opportunity.setEvidenceMomentum(evidence.evidenceMomentum());
2575:         opportunity.setLastBearishAt(evidence.lastBearishAt());
2576:         opportunity.setAverageSignalScore(evidence.averageScore());
2577:         opportunity.setAverageConfidence(evidence.averageConfidence());
2578:         opportunity.setFiveMinuteDecision(evidence.fiveMinute() == null ? null : evidence.fiveMinute().name());
2579:         opportunity.setOneHourDecision(evidence.oneHour() == null ? null : evidence.oneHour().name());
2580:         opportunity.setExecutionSource(source);
2581:         opportunity.setRecommendedPositionPercent(positionPercent);
2582:         opportunity.setDecisionCode(code);
2583:         opportunity.setDecisionExplanation(explanation);

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/service/TradeExecutionValidationService.java:350-415 =====
 350:     private ValidationResult validateBaseSignal(TradeSignal signal, boolean buy) {
 351:         if (signal == null || signal.getGeneratedAt() == null) {
 352:             return ValidationResult.reject("INVALID_SIGNAL", "Execution signal is missing required timing data.");
 353:         }
 354:         if (!EXECUTION_INTERVAL.equals(signal.getInterval())) {
 355:             return ValidationResult.reject("NON_EXECUTION_TIMEFRAME",
 356:                     "Only 1m signals may trigger normal wallet execution. " + signal.getInterval() + " is context-only.");
 357:         }
 358:         if (buy && !isBullish(signal.getDecision())) {
 359:             return ValidationResult.reject("NOT_BUY", "The 1m decision is not BUY/STRONG_BUY.");
 360:         }
 361:         if (!buy && !isBearish(signal.getDecision())) {
 362:             return ValidationResult.reject("NOT_SELL", "The 1m decision is not SELL/STRONG_SELL.");
 363:         }
 364:         return ValidationResult.allow(100, "BASE_VALID", "Base execution signal is valid.");
 365:     }

 368:         return latestSignal(signal.getSymbol(), interval, signal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);

 385:                 && !signal.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(reference.minus(maximumAge));

  28:         SEEDS.put("NEWS_API", new Seed("NewsAPI", "NEWS_API_KEY", 300));
  29:         SEEDS.put("REDDIT", new Seed("Reddit", "REDDIT_ACCESS_TOKEN", 600));

  31:         SEEDS.put("FEAR_GREED", new Seed("Fear & Greed", null, 900));
  32:         SEEDS.put("BINANCE_ANNOUNCEMENT", new Seed("Binance Announcements", null, 300));
  33:         SEEDS.put("WHALE_ALERT", new Seed("Whale Alert", "WHALE_ALERT_API_KEY", 300));
  34:         SEEDS.put("MANUAL_NEWS", new Seed("Manual News", null, 300));

  36: 
  37:     @Override
  38:     @Transactional
  39:     public void run(String... args) {
  40:         for (Map.Entry<String, Seed> entry : SEEDS.entrySet()) {
  41:             String code = entry.getKey();
  42:             if (repository.findByProviderCodeIgnoreCase(code).isPresent()) continue;
  43:             String propertyKey = code.toLowerCase();
  44:             SentimentProperties.Provider configured = properties.provider(propertyKey);
  45:             Seed seed = entry.getValue();
  46:             repository.save(SentimentProviderConfig.builder()
  47:                     .providerCode(code)
  48:                     .displayName(seed.displayName())
  49:                     .enabled(configured.enabled())
  50:                     .weight(configured.weight())
  51:                     .collectionIntervalSeconds(seed.defaultIntervalSeconds())
  52:                     .lastStatus("NEVER_RUN")
  53:                     .apiKeyEnvVar(seed.apiKeyEnvVar())
  54:                     .build());
  55:         }
  56:     }
  57: 
  58:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
  59:     public List<SentimentProviderConfig> findAll() {
  60:         return repository.findAllByOrderByDisplayNameAsc();
  61:     }
  62: 
  63:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
  64:     public SentimentProviderConfig require(String provider) {
  65:         return repository.findByProviderCodeIgnoreCase(SentimentProviderName.normalize(provider))
  66:                 .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("Unknown sentiment provider: " + provider));
  67:     }
  68: 
  69:     @Transactional
  70:     public SentimentProviderConfig update(String provider, SentimentProviderUpdateRequest request) {
  71:         SentimentProviderConfig config = require(provider);
  72:         if (request.enabled() != null) config.setEnabled(request.enabled());
  73:         if (request.weight() != null) config.setWeight(request.weight().max(BigDecimal.ZERO));
  74:         if (request.collectionIntervalSeconds() != null) {
  75:             config.setCollectionIntervalSeconds(Math.max(60, request.collectionIntervalSeconds()));
  76:         }
  77:         return repository.save(config);
  78:     }
  79: 
  80:     @Transactional
  81:     public void recordResult(String provider, String status, String message, boolean success) {
  82:         SentimentProviderConfig config = require(provider);
  83:         Instant now = Instant.now();
  84:         config.setLastCollectionAt(now);
  85:         if (success) config.setLastSuccessAt(now);
  86:         config.setLastStatus(status);
  87:         config.setLastMessage(message == null ? null : message.substring(0, Math.min(1000, message.length())));
  88:         repository.save(config);
  89:     }

## FINDINGS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** RPR-0.6 cannot receive the required dual-agent specification approval: governance requires the identical approved revision at `agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md`, but that file remains RPR-0.4 while RPR-0.6 says it supersedes prior versions “for review only.” [Governance](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/AGENTS.md:327) [RPR-0.6](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION_RPR-0.6.md:3) [canonical artifact](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md:3)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The “complete” registry is materially incomplete. It names nonexistent `regression_test_run` instead of `analysis_test_run`, omits `RegressionTestService`, the request/controller paths, run cancellation/archive/purge, Investigation Queue propagation, and current display-only Production overlays. [RPR-0.6](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION_RPR-0.6.md:48) [actual schema](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/resources/db/migration/V50__create_ai_regression_test_framework.sql:1) [run lifecycle](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestService.java:173)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Isolation is not fully specified. Current Replay reads `wallet_settings` directly in both shadow execution and the experimental observer; shared buy validation also reads it. Replay can call Binance HTTP for minimum-notional checks. RPR-0.6 identifies some of this but does not define the concrete port graph, async propagation, or all affected callers. [Shadow wallet read](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ShadowProductionReplayService.java:999) [shared validation wallet read](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/TradeExecutionValidationService.java:400) [Binance HTTP dependency](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/wallet/service/BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.java:29)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** RPR-0.6 omits material analysis inputs from its read/evidence contract. `AnalysisService` consumes as-of sentiment and fundamentals; sentiment aggregation also reads current mutable provider configuration. The draft permits neither `sentiment_signal` nor `market_fundamental`, and defines no historical provider/config snapshot. This prevents a rigorous `ANALYSIS_PARITY` claim. [Analysis inputs](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/AnalysisService.java:227) [sentiment aggregation/config](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/SentimentService.java:167)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The qualifier/staging contract is incomplete. Today `start()` writes a run and FIX-122 revision before async execution; the worker then writes signals during generation before checking price evidence. RPR-0.6 neither registers the start paths nor defines how profile/mode reach them. Its “validate every input before a generated artifact” rule also lacks a design for position-dependent price coverage. [pre-worker writes](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestService.java:210) [writes before evidence check](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestWorker.java:174) [late evidence check](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestWorker.java:195)

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** The revision must choose and specify either a pure/dry-run qualification phase, full-window coverage, or a two-phase qualification whose failure discards staging and downgrades/refuses the claim. As written, M04 does not define staging tables, promotion atomicity, crash/cancel cleanup, archival, retention, or terminal diagnostic semantics.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** `closed=1` proves only the row’s present state, not that it was closed/visible when Production made a historical decision. Current candle projections omit provenance and write timestamps; later recovery can finalise an open row or insert a closed backfill. [projection](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/repository/CandleRepository.java:23) [recovery finalisation](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/repository/CandleRepository.java:132) [outcome timestamp available only in V108](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/resources/db/migration/V108__fix_153_local_candle_reconciliation.sql:31)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Price-event evidence cannot meet §3’s retained-ID/ordering-slot requirement with the registered changes. The SQL orders by ID, but `PriceEvent` discards both ID and ordering slot; `MarketPriceEventService` is absent from the registry. [schema slot](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/resources/db/migration/V106__fix_153_local_candle_evidence.sql:5) [projection loss](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/market/service/MarketPriceEventService.java:118)

- **AGENT INFERENCE — correction:** The inherited claim that Replay’s timestamp-only re-sort necessarily reverses equal-time ordering was too strong: its immediate input query is ordered by `observed_at, id`. The material defect is loss of the required durable identity/slot and therefore inability to record or prove the promised ordering.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** `ANALYSIS_PARITY` has no earned-claim algorithm. The existing validator compares only selected signal fields and has no Production caller; RPR-0.6 does not define mandatory version/config fingerprints, compared fields, or refusal conditions. [validator scope](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ReplayParityValidator.java:10) [draft’s undeveloped label](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION_RPR-0.6.md:21)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The draft contradicts the handoff constraint that Production code must remain unchanged: it registers changes to shared Production services.
